The results highlight the complexity of the task: while our spatio-temporal model demonstrates high precision (up to 98.2% for +-1 year detection on MTBS and up to 71.3% on the CONUS validation datasets, with F1-scores up to 75.8% and 47.3%, respectively) and effectively reduces spatial artifacts, it exhibits performance trade-offs across different disturbance regimes compared to pixel-wise baselines. Our method offers a promising foundation for consistent forest monitoring.

</details>


### [73] [Geometric-Aware Hypergraph Reasoning for Novel Class Discovery in Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07280)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han,Jialie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对点云分割中的新类发现任务，本文提出超图框架建模高阶类间关联，并结合几何感知原型增强空间结构表征，在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS数据集上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖成对关联进行类别分配和新类推理，难以捕捉已知类与新类之间的复杂关系，同时忽视了点云几何信息，导致语义分割不准确。

Method: (1) 超图框架：建模类间高阶关联，实现从已知类到新类的协同推理；(2) 几何感知原型：利用几何信息增强类别级空间结构的表征，并通过超边传播几何信息。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS两个数据集上，实验验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 超图建模高阶关联与几何感知原型相结合，能够有效提升点云新类发现与分割的准确性。

Abstract: Novel class discovery in point cloud segmentation aims to transfer knowledge from known classes to automatically identify and segment unlabeled novel classes in point clouds. Existing methods mainly rely on pairwise associations for class assignment and novel class reasoning, which limits their ability to capture complex relationships among known and novel classes and may lead to inaccurate semantic segmentation. To address this issue, we introduce a hypergraph-based framework that models high-order associations among classes and enables collaborative reasoning from known classes to novel classes beyond traditional pairwise relations. Moreover, existing methods tend to focus on semantic feature extraction while paying insufficient attention to geometric information in point clouds. To better exploit spatial structure, we propose Geometric-Aware Prototypes to enhance the representation of class-level geometric cues. By propagating geometric information through hyperedges, the proposed method improves the understanding of spatial distributions across classes and leads to more accurate segmentation. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and SemanticPOSS datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method.

</details>


### [74] [ExMesh: EXplicit Mesh Reconstruction with Topology Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07288)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExMesh是一个直接优化显式网格的框架，通过将可微优化与离散拓扑更新相结合，采用自适应的顶点分裂与合并策略及实时UV维护，实现了从粗到细的网格重建，在精度、计算效率和网格简洁性之间取得了平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多视图图像重建表面网格的方法大多依赖中间表示和后处理步骤（如Marching Cubes或TSDF融合），导致伪影和几何碎片化。直接优化显式网格虽具潜力，但面临两个关键挑战：如何自适应细化网格拓扑以捕捉细节而不引入退化面，以及如何在网格结构演化时保持一致的UV坐标以实现高质量纹理映射。

Method: 提出自适应顶点分裂与合并策略，配合实时UV维护机制，将离散拓扑操作无缝集成到连续可微优化管线中，实现从粗到细的网格优化。

Result: 实验证明ExMesh在精度、计算效率和网格简洁性三个方面均取得了优良的平衡。

Conclusion: ExMesh是首个将离散拓扑操作无缝集成到连续可微优化管线中的框架，有效解决了直接优化显式网格的两大核心挑战。

Abstract: Reconstructing surface meshes from multi-view images has remained a core challenge in recent years. Most existing methods, whether implicit or explicit, depend on intermediate representations and post-processing steps like Marching Cubes or TSDF fusion, often resulting in artifacts and fragmented geometry. Directly optimizing explicit meshes is a promising approach. However, it presents two critical challenges. The first is how to adaptively refine mesh topology to capture detail without introducing degenerate faces. The second is how to maintain consistent UV coordinates for high-fidelity texturing as the mesh structure evolves. To overcome these, we propose ExMesh, a novel framework that directly optimizes explicit meshes by integrating differentiable optimization with discrete topology updates. Specifically, we introduce an adaptive vertex splitting and merging strategy, along with real-time UV maintenance, to enable coarse-to-fine optimization while preserving geometric integrity. To our knowledge, ExMesh is the first framework to seamlessly integrate discrete topology operations into a continuous differentiable optimization pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ExMesh achieves a balance among accuracy, computational efficiency, and mesh conciseness.

</details>


### [75] [CULTURESCORE: Evaluating Cultural Faithfulness in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07311)
*Anku Rani,Wei Dai,Shravan Nayak,Pattie Maes,Mahdi M. Kalayeh,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CultureScore，一个将文化忠实度分解为身份、背景、行为三个维度的评估框架。对10个国家、3个SOTA模型、6180个生成视频的评估显示，最佳模型仅达56.8%的CultureScore，且视觉质量最高的模型在文化忠实度上排名最差，表明现有视频生成模型存在严重的文化失真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估指标（如VideoScore）仅衡量视觉质量，无法评估生成内容的文化忠实度。例如，将合十礼替换为握手的模型与生成正确手势的模型得分相同，这导致模型可能系统性忽视或扭曲非西方文化表征。

Method: 提出CultureScore，一个将文化忠实度分解为三个维度的组合式评估框架：Identity（表征了谁）、Context（文化背景是否准确）、Behavior（规范性手势与互动）。选取10个国家，使用3个SOTA视频生成模型（如Veo 3.1、LTX-2）生成了6180个视频进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型仅获得56.8%的CultureScore；Behavior维度最具挑战性，所有模型在此维度得分均低于52%；人类偏好排名与CultureScore方向一致，但与VideoScore呈反向关系——视觉质量最高的模型在文化忠实度上被标注者评为最差。

Conclusion: 当前所有视频生成模型均无法实现文化忠实的内容生成，文化忠实度是公平视频生成的必要条件，而非可选的附加指标。CultureScore为评估和改善生成模型的文化表征能力提供了可行框架。

Abstract: As video generation models like Veo 3.1 and LTX-2 advance, their ability to accurately represent diverse global cultures remains a critical yet understudied frontier. Current metrics, such as VideoScore, only measure visual quality but offer no mechanism for assessing cultural faithfulness. Consequently, a model that replaces a Namaste with a handshake receives the same score as one that generates the gesture correctly. We propose CultureScore, a compositional evaluation framework that decomposes cultural faithfulness into three granular dimensions: Identity (who is represented), Context (culturally localized background), and Behavior (normative gestures and interactions). We operationalize this framework through an evaluation suite spanning 10 countries, yielding 6,180 generated videos across three state-of-the-art models. Our evaluation reveals that no current model achieves culturally faithful video generation: the best-performing model reaches only 56.8\% overall CultureScore, with Behavior the most challenging dimension, which remains below 52\% across all models. Furthermore, human preference rankings align directionally with CultureScore but are inverted relative to VideoScore; the highest-scoring model on visual quality was ranked last by annotators, underscoring that cultural faithfulness is an essential criterion for equitable video generation.

</details>


### [76] [AnchorWorld: Embodied Egocentric World Simulation with View-based Evolution Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07326)
*Yu Li,Menghan Xia,Gongye Liu,Xintao Wang,Conglang Zhang,Lei Ke,Yuxuan Lin,Ruihang Chu,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorWorld是一个交互式世界建模框架，通过引入外源视角辅助训练实现全身姿态感知，并基于锚点视图和文本描述实现可定制的自演化世界仿真，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界建模在实际应用中需要灵活的 controllability，但目前缺乏对多功能可控制性的探索。特别是第一人称视角下身体部位被截断或超出视野，导致交互的空间 grounding 不鲁棒。

Method: 1) 以3D人体运动作为主要交互模态；2) 引入与第一人称视角解耦的外源视角作为辅助训练监督，使模型能观察到完整身体相对环境的定位；3) 提出锚点视图机制，在统一世界坐标系中定义锚点视图，结合文本描述控制局部场景的动态演化。

Result: AnchorWorld在多个指标上显著优于当前最先进的基线方法；消融实验验证了外源视角监督和锚点视图定制机制的有效性；定制方案展现出良好的时空几何一致性，并严格遵守指定的演化动态。

Conclusion: 本工作推进了第一人称交互式世界建模，通过外源视角增强空间 grounding 和锚点视图实现灵活定制，为实际场景中的可控世界仿真提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Despite being a pivotal frontier, interactive world modeling remains underexplored in terms of the versatile controllability required by practical scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present AnchorWorld, a framework that advances egocentric simulation through enhanced interaction integrity and a flexible mechanism for world customization. First, we utilize 3D human motion as the primary interaction modality. To complement the out-of-view or truncated body parts in egocentric views, we introduce an auxiliary training supervision that incorporates exogenous viewpoints decoupled from the agent's first-person sensorium. It allows the model to observe the agent's full-body positioning relative to the environment, facilitating a more robust spatial grounding of human-world interactions. Furthermore, we propose a simple yet effective mechanism for customizing self-evolving worlds. This is achieved by defining anchor views within a unified world coordinate system, coupled with textual descriptions dictating the dynamic evolution of local scenes. Experimental results show that AnchorWorld significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, while ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our key designs. Notably, our customization scheme exhibits promising spatio-temporal geometric consistency and adheres strictly to the prescribed evolutionary dynamics.

</details>


### [77] [Varifold Moment Invariants for Sustainable and Explainable Contour Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07333)
*G. Longari,J. -C. Alvarez Paiva,A. B. Tumpach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI)框架，统一了多种矩不变量方法，通过结合区域几何、边界及切线族生成高判别力的几何特征，配合轻量分类器在多种数据集上以更低计算成本实现超越现有方法的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩不变量方法分散多样，缺乏统一理论框架；同时需要在不牺牲判别力的前提下降低计算成本，使其能够在轻量设备上运行。

Method: 提出Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI)框架，融合区域几何、边界以及切线族的信息；耦合轻量分类器Random Forest或MLP进行特征提取与分类。

Result: 在树叶、物体、细胞等多种广泛使用的数据集上，以较少的几何可解释特征实现了高精度分类，计算成本大幅降低，可在轻量设备上运行。

Conclusion: VMI框架统一了多种矩不变量方法，具有高判别力和清晰几何意义，且计算效率高，适合轻量级应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI) as a unifying framework for many previously introduced Moment Invariants. These invariants are deeply related to other contour features that are invariant under translations and rotations, like Extended Gaussian Image, Elliptic Fourier Descriptors or Shape Distributions. The advantage of the varifold approach to moments consists in being able to combine the geometry of the region, its boundary, and the family of lines tangent to it, in order to create a substantial number of invariant features with high discriminating power and clear geometric meaning. By coupling our VMI feature extraction with the light feature classifiers Random Forest or Multi-Layer-Perceptron, we outperform state-of-the-art approaches based on contours, while decreasing drastically the computational cost to the point of allowing our algorithm to run on light devices. We tested our approach on classification tasks on a large number of widely-used datasets of various types (leaves, objects, cells) and achieved high accuracy with a low number of geometrically interpretable features.

</details>


### [78] [VeriDrive: Verifiable Counterfactual Supervision for Cost-Efficient Vision-Language Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07338)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. H. Shum,Toby P. Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriDrive 提出了一种结构化的可验证反事实监督框架，通过感知-评估-修正链将驾驶推理转化为可审计的中间表示，在降低生成成本和 token 用量的同时提升了驾驶规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的驾驶推理方法使用自由形式的推理监督，不仅生成成本高（依赖前沿模型），而且难以验证和审计。需要一种结构化、低成本且可验证的驾驶推理监督方案。

Method: VeriDrive 构建了 Perception-Evaluation-Revision（感知-评估-修正）链：1）将关键物体锚定到未来运动；2）用基于规则的验真器评估替代自车轨迹；3）将风险意图修正为专家行为；4）产生最终规划目标。通过本地生成结合验证器引导的选择性修正来扩展数据构建，仅对无效或困难样本进行升级处理。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上构建数据集并在 Omni-Q 协议下训练，开环实验显示 VeriDrive 在 L2、碰撞率和 Intersection 指标上优于 OmniDrive，同时降低了 token 用量、生成时间和实际 LLM/VLM API 成本。

Conclusion: 可审计的中间字段和结构化修正目标能够在现实的标注预算约束下改进视觉-语言规划监督的质量。

Abstract: Vision-language driving models increasingly use reasoning supervision to bridge perception, prediction, and planning, but existing driving rationales are often free-form and expensive to generate with frontier models. We present VeriDrive, a framework for constructing planning-oriented, verifiable counterfactual supervision. VeriDrive converts driving reasoning into a structured Perception-Evaluation-Revision chain that grounds key objects in future motion, evaluates alternative ego trajectories with rule-checkable evidence, revises risky intent toward expert behavior, and produces final planning targets. To scale data construction, VeriDrive combines local generation with validator-guided selective correction, escalating only invalid or difficult samples. We build the VeriDrive dataset on nuScenes and train under the Omni-Q protocol. Controlled open-loop experiments show that VeriDrive improves L2, Collision, and Intersection over OmniDrive while reducing logged token usage, generation time, and actual paid LLM/VLM cost. These results show that auditable intermediate fields and structured revision targets can improve vision-language planning supervision under realistic annotation budgets. Code, prompts, and validator scripts are coming soon and will be released after the review process.

</details>


### [79] [Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter for Micro-gesture Online Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07355)
*Xucheng Shen,Kun Li,Fei Wang,Wei Qian,Jin Jiang,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空解耦适配器（STDA）和自适应软平衡增强（ASBA）方法，用于微手势在线识别，在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中取得第一名（F1=0.43808）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势具有极短时长、低运动幅度和模糊视觉线索的特点，现有参数高效适配器采用单分支联合建模时空特征，难以捕获微手势的细粒度模式。

Method: 1) 时空解耦适配器（STDA）：将视频适配分解为独立的时间分支和空间分支，通过轻量级深度可分离卷积分别建模时空特征。2) 自适应软平衡增强（ASBA）：根据类别稀有度和学习难度动态分配增强强度，无需手动阈值，解决长尾分布问题。

Result: 在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中取得F1分数0.43808，排名第1。

Conclusion: 时空解耦策略和自适应增强方法有效提升了微手势识别的性能，尤其是在处理细粒度时空特征和类别不平衡问题上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Micro-gesture online recognition aims to temporally localize and classify subtle gestures in untrimmed videos. Owing to their extremely short duration, low motion amplitude, and ambiguous visual cues, capturing discriminative spatiotemporal representations remains highly challenging. Existing parameter-efficient adapters typically employ a single branch to model spatial and temporal cues jointly, which may fail to capture the fine-grained patterns of micro-gestures. To address this limitation, we propose a Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter that decomposes video adaptation into independent temporal and spatial branches via lightweight depthwise convolutions. In addition, to address the long-tail distribution problem in the benchmark dataset, we introduce Adaptive Soft Balanced Augmentation, which dynamically allocates augmentation intensity based on class rarity and learning difficulty, without manual thresholds. Our method achieves an F1 score of 0.43808, ranking 1st in Track 2 of the 4th EI-MiGA-IJCAI Challenge.

</details>


### [80] [Mitosis Detection in the Wild: Multi-Tumor and Context-Aware Generalization in the MIDOG 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07368)
*Marc Aubreville,Jonas Ammeling,Sweta Banerjee,Viktoria Weiss,Taryn A. Donovan,Robert Klopfleisch,Jiaqi Lv,Shan E Ahmed Raza,Raphaël Bourgade,Thomas Walter,Yasemin Topuz,Songül Varlı,Charles-Antoine Collins-Fekete,Zhuoyan Shen,Navya Sri Kelam,Nitin Singhal,Christian Marzahl,Brian Napora,Tengyou Xu,Hongyan Gu,Mario Vento,Gennaro Percannella,Norbert Ropiak,Izabela Wasiak,Jie Xiao,Shaojun Liu,Seungho Choe,April Khademi,Vidushi Walia,Sujatha Kotte,Andrew Broad,Alex Wright,Guillaume Balezo,Esha Sadia Nasir,Mostafa Jahanifar,Yosuke Yamagishi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Mattia Sarno,Francesco Tortorella,Biwen Meng,Jingxin Liu,Sara Krauss,Daniel Hieber,Lavish Ramchandani,Dev Kumar Das,Mieko Ochi,Yuan Bae,Piotr Giedziun,Mateusz Maniewski,Vangala Govindakrishnan Saipradeep,Naveen Sivadasan,Leire Benito-Del-Valle,Adrian Galdran,Kaustubh Atey,Sameer Anand Jha,Adinath Dukre,Imran Razzak,Maxime W. Lafarge,Viktor H. Koelzer,Nils Porsche,Nikolas Stathonikos,Mitko Veta,Dominik Hirling,Zsanett Zsófia Iván,Peter Horvath,Katharina Breininger,Christof A. Bertram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIDOG 2025挑战构建了一个涵盖12种人、犬、猫肿瘤类型、365例病例的多域泛化测试集，评估有丝分裂检测算法在热点区域、随机区域和困难区域的表现，并引入非典型有丝分裂图（AMF）分类任务。检测赛道最优F1达0.740，AMF赛道平衡精度最高0.908；模型在困难ROI的假阳性率翻了三倍，且在不同肿瘤类型间表现差异显著；集成策略平均提升F1 1.5%、平衡精度1.3%，而TTA无显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有丝分裂检测基准主要关注扫描仪引起的域偏移，但临床实际应用要求模型在跨肿瘤类型、跨物种、跨组织学背景的巨大差异下保持鲁棒性。MIDOG 2025旨在填补这一空白，建立更接近真实世界临床场景的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含365例病例、12种肿瘤类型（人、犬、猫）、多扫描平台的测试集；设置三个评估场景：传统热点区域、全切片随机区域、困难区域（富含难负样本）；第二赛道引入非典型有丝分裂图（AMF）分类；评估集成和测试时增强（TTA）策略的效果。

Result: 检测赛道18支队伍，最高F1=0.740；AMF赛道21支队伍，最高平衡精度=0.908；困难ROI中假阳性率翻三倍；12种肿瘤类型间性能差异显著；集成策略平均提升F1 1.5%、平衡精度1.3%；TTA无显著改进。

Conclusion: "野外"有丝分裂检测仍是重大挑战。从仅热点区域评估转向多上下文评估框架（热点、随机区域、困难区域）为临床可靠性提供了更现实的代理指标；揭示了当前SOTA架构在罕见或高度多形性恶性肿瘤上的"盲点"。

Abstract: Automated mitosis detection is a well-established task in computational pathology. While previous benchmarks focused on scanner-induced domain shift, clinical "real-world" application requires models to be robust across the vast variance to be expected in the histological landscape. The MItosis DOmain Generalization (MIDOG) 2025 challenge was designed to evaluate algorithmic performance across unprecedented biological and contextual diversity. We curated a test dataset of 365 cases, encompassing 12 distinct human, canine and feline tumor types, digitized across multiple scanning platforms. Moving beyond hand-selected hotspots, the challenge required detection also in random tissue areas (representative of the whole slide detection situation) and challenging areas (areas rich in hard negatives). In the second track, we introduced the classification of atypical mitotic figures (AMFs). There were 18 teams submitting to the detection track, with F1 scores ranging up to 0.740. In the AMF detection track, we had 21 submissions with balanced accuracy values up to 0.908. Our analysis reveals that while most models perform reliably in traditional hotspots, significant performance degradation occurs in challenging ROIs, where false positive rates tripled. Furthermore, performance varied significantly across the 12 tumor types, highlighting "blind spots" in current state-of-the-art architectures when encountering rare or highly pleomorphic malignancies. Moreover, we evaluated the effectiveness of ensembling and found a mean increases of 1.5 and 1.3 percentage points in F1 score and balanced accuracy, respectively. In contrast, TTA showed no relevant improvement. MIDOG 2025 demonstrates that "in the wild" mitosis detection remains a significant hurdle. The transition from hotspot-only evaluation to a multi-contextual framework provides a more realistic proxy for clinical reliability.

</details>


### [81] [Mind the Gap: Disentangling Performance Bottlenecks in Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07394)
*Danial Hamdi,Fardin Ayar,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于整数线性规划（ILP）的诊断框架，通过层次化隔离分类、分割和跟踪三种误差源，对七种视频实例分割（VIS）方法进行分析，发现跟踪不稳定性是主要瓶颈，尤其在严重遮挡、视频长度增加和实例密度增大时表现突出。同时引入可视化工具TrackLens辅助分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频实例分割（VIS）中分类、分割和跟踪三个目标被联合评估，但各自对性能损失的个体贡献不透明，缺乏系统化的诊断方法来隔离和分析不同误差来源。

Method: 提出了一个模型无关的诊断框架，将身份分配和类别分配形式化为整数线性规划（ILP）问题，层次化地隔离分类、分割和跟踪三种误差源。同时引入可视分析工具TrackLens，将误差幅度映射为可观察的查询级失败模式。

Result: 对七种VIS方法（涵盖在线和离线范式）在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021及OVIS诊断子集上的分析表明：跟踪不稳定性是在线方法的关键瓶颈，严重遮挡下AP差距超过20，且随视频长度和实例密度增加而急剧增大；语义分类在标准基准上有贡献，但在跟踪失败严重的场景下影响可忽略；更强的骨干网络提升默认分数，但AP跟踪差距基本不变，证实时间脆弱性是算法性的而非纯粹表征性的。

Conclusion: 系统性地揭示了VIS的核心挑战在于鲁棒的长时间跨度的时序关联（跟踪），而非分类或分割，为后续研究提供了诊断工具和方向指引。

Abstract: In Video Instance Segmentation (VIS), classification, segmentation, and tracking objectives are jointly evaluated, but their individual contributions to performance loss remain opaque. We introduce a diagnostic framework that formulates identity and class assignment as an Integer Linear Program (ILP), yielding a model-agnostic oracle that hierarchically isolates each error source. Applied to seven VIS methods spanning online and offline paradigms across YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 and a diagnostic subset of OVIS, our analysis reveals a consistent picture. Tracking instability is a critical bottleneck for online methods, with gaps exceeding 20 AP under heavy occlusion, and grows sharply with video length and instance density. While semantic classification contributes meaningfully on standard benchmarks, its impact becomes negligible where tracking fails most. Although stronger backbones substantially lift default scores, they leave AP tracking gaps largely intact, confirming that temporal fragility is algorithmic rather than purely representational. To complement the oracle, we introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that translates gap magnitude into observable, query-level failure modes. Together, these tools provide a systematic foundation for targeting VIS's core challenge: robust long-term temporal association.

</details>


### [82] [RealDocBench: A Benchmark for Field-Level QA and Layout Understanding on Real-World Regulated Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07401)
*Ameya Joshi,Joon Kim,Gus Eggert,Joseph Bajor,Cindy Hao,Jing Reyhan,Kushal Byatnal,Eli Badgio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDocBench是一个面向真实世界监管文档的双轨基准测试，包含QA和布局两个任务，对18个文档解析系统进行统一评估，揭示了单一指标无法反映的性能差异、成本与延迟的权衡，以及医疗文档子领域的持续性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准多基于干净学术布局或合成文本，仅报告OCR或Markdown层面的单一相似度分数，与下游智能体实际需求（从杂乱真实页面中提取特定字段的正确值）严重脱节。亟需一个反映真实监管文档场景的标准化评估基准。

Method: 构建两个赛道：QA赛道包含4个领域581份文档的1,356个字段级问题，每问配有类型化的gold_dict键值对答案，采用逐字段和严格逐问题两种精度评分；布局赛道包含1,500张人工验证的页面图像，标注COCO格式九类边界框，采用包含邻域感知分割/合并恢复的匈牙利匹配器评分。在统一提取-评分协议下评估18个系统（商业解析API、通用VLM、开源OCR模型），同时报告精度、每页成本和缓存延迟。

Result: RealDocBench暴露了单一数字指标所掩盖的广泛性能差距，发现医疗文档子领域是持续性的困难领域，并揭示了不同操作点之间显著的成本/延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了现有文档解析评估与下游实际需求之间的鸿沟，通过发布数据集、解析适配器和评估框架，支持可复现的字段级文档解析系统比较。

Abstract: Document parsing systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes, regulated workflows such as mortgage underwriting, financial reporting, supply-chain logistics, and clinical records. Yet most public benchmarks evaluate parsers on clean academic layouts or synthetic prose, and report a single OCR or markdown-level similarity score. Such documents and metrics correlate poorly with what downstream agents actually need: the correct value for a specific field on a messy real-world page. We introduce RealDocBench, a two-track benchmark built from real regulated documents. The QA track contains 1,356 field-level questions over 581 documents spanning four domains, where each question is paired with a typed gold_dict of key-to-value answers and parsers are scored on both per-field and strict per-question accuracy. The layout track contains 1,500 human-verified page images annotated with COCO-style bounding boxes under a nine-class public taxonomy, scored with a Hungarian matcher that includes adjacency-aware split/merge recovery. We evaluate eighteen systems, spanning commercial parsing APIs, general-purpose VLMs, and open-source OCR models, under a uniform extraction-and-scoring protocol, and report accuracy alongside per-page cost and cache-busted latency. RealDocBench exposes a wide performance spread that single-number benchmarks hide, a persistently hard medical sub-domain, and sharp cost/latency trade-offs across operating points. We release the datasets, parser adapters, and evaluation harness to support reproducible, field-level comparison of document parsing systems.

</details>


### [83] [DisPOSE: Projected Polystochastic Diffusion for Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07419)
*Tony Danjun Wang,Tolga Birdal,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisPOSE是一个自监督框架，通过将多视角多人3D姿态估计中的分配问题建模为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程，并结合可微Sinkhorn投影和超图卷积解码器，实现了对遮挡场景下多人3D姿态的高效鲁棒重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖合成3D姿态目录，在真实场景中因分布偏移泛化能力差；多视角多人姿态估计中的人-视角分配问题本质上是离散组合优化问题，难以端到端学习。

Method: 1) 将多视角多人分配问题建模为多随机张量空间上的扩散生成过程；2) 在去噪过程中使用可微Sinkhorn投影，引导解向有效可行分配收敛；3) 通过超图卷积解码器从多视角特征回归完整3D骨架，显式建模关节点间关系结构。

Result: 在标准数据集上超越当前最优自监督方法；在高度遮挡的手术室场景新基准上表现强劲；仅用10%伪标签即可保留99%性能，标签效率极高；对相机排布近乎无关。

Conclusion: 将分配与根节点回归解耦并保持可微性，是DisPOSE对相机排布鲁棒的关键；扩散式定位方法具有高标签效率和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human poses for multiple individuals from different camera views is a fundamental bottleneck for analyzing interacting behaviors. Existing self-supervised approaches leverage synthetic catalogues of 3D poses; however, this leads to poor generalization in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts. We therefore introduce DisPOSE, a self-supervised framework that approximates the inherently discrete multi-view person-assignment problem as a generative diffusion process over the space of polystochastic tensors. By employing differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, our model learns to guide solutions toward valid and feasible assignments based on 2D image priors. The complete 3D skeletons of localized individuals are then regressed using a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that explicitly models relational structures and articulated joints across multiple views. The proposed approach outperforms current state-of-the-art self-supervised methods on standard datasets and demonstrates strong performance on a newly proposed benchmark featuring highly occluded scenes from surgical operating rooms. Our diffusion-based localization demonstrates high label efficiency, retaining 99% of its performance with only 10% of the pseudo-labels. Notably, disentangling the assignment and root regression components while maintaining differentiability makes DisPOSE nearly agnostic to different camera arrangements.

</details>


### [84] [OpenGlass: Open-Source Smart Glasses for On-Device Event-Based Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07431)
*Pietro Bonazzi,Julian Moosmann,Ahmet Celik,Philipp Mayer,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源智能眼镜平台，通过模块化硬件设计（柔性FPC转接板支持事件/帧相机）、协同电源管理（nRF5340唤醒+GAP9 SoC间歇工作）实现11.8小时连续ML推理，并在LynX数据集上以R(2+1)D模型达到83.94%的手势识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜受限于功耗、内存和计算资源，且支持事件视觉和嵌入式ML的开源硬件平台稀缺，亟需一个模块化、低功耗的开源平台来快速原型验证新型传感器和算法。

Method: 提出模块化智能眼镜平台：1）采用柔性FPC转接板支持事件/帧相机切换；2）硬件-软件协同电源管理，nRF5340协处理器事件驱动唤醒GAP9 RISC-V SoC；3）手势识别采用极性分离事件直方图作为输入，在GAP9上部署R(2+1)D三维卷积网络。

Result: 200mAh电池下连续ML推理续航达11.8小时；在LynX数据集上R(2+1)D模型跨被试留二验证准确率83.94%（宏F1=0.781），GAP9上端到端延迟33.9ms；时间增强和去除模糊类别带来最大提升（+8.9百分点）。

Conclusion: 该开源平台证明了在极端资源受限的智能眼镜上实现持续事件视觉ML的可行性，所有设计、固件和模型均已开源，为智能眼镜研究提供了可复现的模块化基准平台。

Abstract: Smart eyewear enables unobtrusive, context-aware interaction through multimodal sensors and on-device intelligence, but is severely limited by power, memory, and compute constraints in a compact form factor. Open-hardware platforms supporting event-based vision and embedded ML at this scale are rare. This work introduces an open-source smart glasses platform for rapid prototyping of novel sensors and algorithms. Its modular design uses a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras without full PCB redesign. A hardware-software co-designed power management system combines a configurable PMIC with event-driven wake-up via an nRF5340 coordinator, keeping the GAP9 RISC-V SoC powered down between inferences. The prototype achieves up to 11.8 hours of continuous on-device ML from a 200 mAh battery. As a demonstration, an egocentric hand gesture recognition pipeline was evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee GENX320 camera. R(2+1)D achieved the best cross-subject accuracy of 83.94\% (macro F1 = 0.781) under leave-two-subjects-out validation, with 33.9 ms end-to-end latency on the GAP9. Temporal augmentation and removal of ambiguous classes provided the largest gains (+8.9 pp). All hardware designs, firmware, and models are released open source.

</details>


### [85] [Watch, Remember, Reason: Human-View Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07433)
*Jiahao Meng,Yue Tan,Qi Xu,Kuan Gao,Weisong Liu,Yanwei Li,Jason Li,Lingdong Kong,Haochen Wang,Qianyu Zhou,Jiangning Zhang,Guangliang Cheng,Yunhai Tong,Lu Qi,Minghsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从人类视角（watching/remembering/reasoning）系统综述了基于多模态大语言模型的视频理解研究，涵盖感知、记忆、推理三大能力，梳理了代表性方法、应用领域、数据集与基准，并指出了未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解研究正从短片段向长视频、多模态、知识密集型场景演进，现有工作零散分布于不同基准任务，缺乏统一的分析框架。本文旨在以人类认知视角（观看、记住、推理）构建结构化视角，系统分析视频MLLM在稀疏证据、长程依赖、多模态对齐及有限计算下的能力与挑战。

Method: 提出一个形式化框架，将视频理解系统分解为感知表示、记忆状态、推理痕迹和最终预测四个组件。围绕watching（细粒度、全面、音视频、高效感知）、remembering（离线与流式记忆）、reasoning（纯文本推理与视频思维）三大功能组织代表性方法，并覆盖应用领域、数据集与基准。

Result: 系统梳理了视频MLLM在时空感知、高效长视频处理、记忆建模、流式理解、忠实推理等方面的挑战与现有方法，归纳了不同任务类型、监督格式、模态和能力维度下的数据集与评估基准。

Conclusion: 指出开放问题与未来方向：可扩展的、记忆感知的、基于证据的视频智能，并持续跟踪相关工作。

Abstract: Video understanding is being rapidly transformed by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as research moves from short clips to long, multimodal, and knowledge-intensive video scenarios. These scenarios require models to handle sparse evidence, long-range dependencies, multimodal alignment, and reliable inference under limited computational budgets. This work presents a human-view perspective on LLM-based video understanding, organized around three functional abilities: watching, remembering, and reasoning. Rather than treating video tasks as isolated benchmarks, this view provides a unified structure for analyzing how video MLLMs acquire evidence, preserve context, and produce grounded outputs. We introduce a formulation that characterizes video understanding systems by their perceptual representations, memory states, reasoning traces, and final predictions. Based on this formulation, we identify challenges in spatio-temporal perception, efficient long-video processing, memory modeling, streaming understanding, and faithful reasoning. Representative methods are organized by their roles in video MLLM systems. Watching covers fine-grained, comprehensive, audio-visual, and efficient perception. Remembering includes offline and streaming memory, while reasoning covers text-only reasoning and thinking with videos. We further examine application domains such as egocentric, sports, instructional, medical, and narrative videos, and cover training datasets and evaluation benchmarks across task types, supervision formats, modalities, and capability dimensions. Finally, we outline open problems and future directions for scalable, memory-aware, and evidence-grounded video intelligence. Related works will be continuously traced at https://github.com/marinero4972/Awesome-HumanView-VideoUnderstanding.

</details>


### [86] [The Lipreading Gap: Do VSR Models Perceive Visual Speech Like Human Lipreaders?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07435)
*Rishabh Jain,Naomi Harte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 虽然VSR模型在整体准确率上超越人类，但它们依赖语言统计线索而非视觉感知，与人类的视觉语音感知机制存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前性能超越人类唇读者的VSR模型是否真正具备了类似人类的视觉语音感知能力。

Method: 在MaFI词级唇读数据集上，使用词、字符、音素、视位素四级指标，比较三种VSR系统与人类基线；引入文本n-gram基线；分析模型错误与训练词频、视觉信息量的关系；比较视位素准确率、混淆矩阵及人机相关性。

Result: 模型整体准确率更高，但成功/失败的词与人类不同；仅给定少量初始音素的文本n-gram基线即可媲美人类唇读；模型词级错误与训练词频相关性更强，与视觉信息量相关性更弱；模型在人类最难识别的视位素上提升最大，对视觉清晰度的依赖远弱于人类。

Conclusion: VSR系统主要依赖训练数据中的语言线索而非视觉感知，未能将视觉特征绑定为有意义的词，与人类的视觉语音感知机制存在本质差异。

Abstract: Visual speech recognition (VSR) models now surpass human lipreaders on benchmarks, but do such gains establish human-like visual speech perception? To explore this, we compare three VSR systems with human baselines on the MaFI word-level lipreading dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme-level metrics. Although models achieve higher overall accuracy, they succeed and fail on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline given only a few initial phonemes rivals human lipreading. VSR word-level errors are consistently better explained by training word frequency than by the visual informativeness of words. Viseme accuracies, confusion matrices and human-model correlations further show that models gain most on visemes humans find hardest, and show much weaker dependence on visual clarity. Our work demonstrates that VSR systems rely primarily on language cues from training data rather than visual perception, failing to bind visual features into meaningful words.

</details>


### [87] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-3D框架，通过场景记忆和自演化技能库，使MLLM智能体在3D空间推理中根据具体场景自适应选择工具，显著提升工具利用率（从39%到78%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D空间推理的MLLM智能体对所有场景采用统一的工具使用策略，导致工具误用和偏好偏差，非智能体策略相比增益有限。作者揭示3D空间推理任务在不同场景间具有异质性，需要场景感知的工具选择。

Method: Skill-3D框架包含：(1) 场景记忆模块，记录智能体在特定场景中的工具使用轨迹；(2) 技能库，将成功轨迹聚合蒸馏为可复用的场景感知技能，失败轨迹作为教训附加；(3) 自演化循环，当相似场景再次出现时注入对应技能指导智能体，新产生的成功/失败轨迹进一步精炼技能，实现记忆与技能库协同演化。

Result: 在VSI-Bench上，工具利用率从39%提升至78%；在MMSI-Bench上，Gemini-3-Flash提升67%；在VSI-Bench上，对Qwen3-VL-8B进行智能体后训练后提升43%。

Conclusion: Skill-3D通过场景感知的自演化技能学习，有效解决了3D空间推理中工具误用和偏好偏差问题，显著提升了智能体工具利用的正确性和充分性，验证了场景自适应策略优于统一策略。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [88] [TEVI: Text-Conditioned Editing of Visual Representations via Sparse Autoencoders for Improved Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07451)
*Sweta Mahajan,Sukrut Rao,Jiahao Xie,Alexander Koller,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEVI是一个利用稀疏自编码器和掩码模块，以文本描述为信号从图像嵌入中选择性保留信息并丢弃无关信息的框架，能够改善CLIP模型的跨模态对齐与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的图像和文本嵌入之间存在信息不平衡：图像包含的信息远多于其文本描述，导致嵌入空间对齐不佳，影响下游任务性能。现有工作虽指出这一问题，但缺乏有效解决方案。

Method: 提出TEVI框架：首先使用稀疏自编码器对图像嵌入进行解耦，然后训练一个掩码模块，根据给定文本描述选择性重建图像嵌入中与描述相关的部分，同时丢弃无关信息。

Result: 在合成描述的控制实验中，TEVI能有效保留描述所涉及的属性并丢弃其他属性。在自然图像上，TEVI在粗粒度短文本（MS COCO, Flickr）和细粒度长文本（IIW, DOCCI）检索基准上均取得改进，且在更丰富的描述上增益更大；同时在RoCOCO鲁棒性基准上表现更优。

Conclusion: TEVI通过以文本描述为导向选择性保留图像嵌入信息，有效缓解了视觉-语言模型中的信息不平衡问题，显著提升了跨模态检索性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP are highly useful for diverse tasks due to their shared image-text embedding space. Despite this, the image and text embeddings are often poorly aligned, affecting downstream performance. Recent work has shown that this can be attributed to an information imbalance: images contain more information than their captions describe. In this work, we propose TEVI, a framework that uses captions as a signal for what to retain from image embeddings. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders to disentangle image embeddings and train a masking module to selectively reconstruct the embedding based on a given caption. In a controlled setup with synthetic captions, we show that TEVI is effective at preserving caption-described attributes while discarding others. By applying TEVI to CLIP models trained on natural images, we further achieve improved retrieval performance across coarse-grained short-caption (MS COCO, Flickr) and fine-grained long-caption (IIW, DOCCI) benchmarks, with stronger gains on richer captions, and improved robustness on the RoCOCO benchmark.

</details>


### [89] [Implicit Data Synthesis for Contrastive Unsupervised Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07498)
*Patrick Kage,Trevor Hedges,N. Siddharth,Pavlos Andreadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过对网络权重而非数据进行扰动来生成对比学习样本的方法，用于处理科学观测数据（如雷达流星观测），在SimCLR框架下验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学观测数据标注成本高，无监督学习（尤其是对比学习）有价值，但传统数据空间增强方法会破坏科学数据的底层结构。

Method: 提出对网络权重进行扰动（而非数据空间增强）来生成对比样本，基于SimCLR流程，应用于雷达流星观测数据。

Result: 在匹配协议下，该方法相比传统数据增强方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 权重扰动对比学习能更好地保持科学数据固有结构，是数据空间增强的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Scientific observations generate large quantities of unlabeled data which is laborious to hand-label, making unsupervised learning techniques valuable for processing datasets. Among these approaches, contrastive learning provides a convenient mechanism for extracting structural representations from unannotated datasets. For natural imagery, the general approach is to use a variety of data-space augmentation methods in order to generate synthetic samples; however, for scientific observations data-space perturbations can fundamentally alter the underlying data. Our proposed method is to generate contrastive samples by perturbing the network weights rather than the underlying data, thus more closely preserving the structure of the data. We demonstrate this technique using a SimCLR-based pipeline applied over radar observations of meteors, and show performance gains under matched protocols.

</details>


### [90] [Differences in Detection: Explainability Where it Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07503)
*Johannes Theodoridis,Johannes Maucher,Andreas Schilling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DnD（检测差异）的方法，用于直接比较两个目标检测模型，通过匹配算法计算它们共同识别、各自独有识别以及共同遗漏的真实标签，并结合错误类型进行混淆矩阵分析，还可引导可解释性方法关注关键样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的mAP和TIDE等标准指标只能提供独立的汇总统计，缺乏直接、直观地比较两个目标检测模型的能力，无法揭示模型间的共同错误和各自特有错误。

Method: 基于相同匹配算法，计算两个模型共同识别的真实标签交集，各自独有的差异集，以及共同遗漏的补集；结合错误类型，在标准混淆矩阵中分析检测错误差异。

Result: 提供了比独立汇总统计更直接和直观的比较结果，能够揭示模型间的共享错误和独特错误，并可用于引导可解释性方法聚焦于指标相关的结构化子集样本。

Conclusion: DnD方法本身具有价值，且其最佳应用之一是引导ODAM等可解释性方法聚焦于结构化子集中的关键样本，从而提升模型分析的针对性和可解释性。

Abstract: We propose Differences in Detection (DnD), an intuitive method to compare two object detection models. Based on the same matching algorithm, it complements the standard metrics of mean Average Precision ($mAP$) and TIDE error analysis with the ability to compare two models directly. More specifically, we calculate the intersection of ground truth labels that are recognized by both models, followed by the corresponding difference sets and the complement set of ground truth labels that are missed by both models. The resulting comparison is more direct and intuitive than a comparison of independent summary statistics. It reveals individual and shared mistakes and becomes particularly interesting when combined with error types. In this case, the differences in detection errors can be analyzed naturally in a standard confusion matrix. While valuable in itself, we believe that one of the best applications of DnD is to guide explainability methods such as ODAM towards metric-relevant examples, grounded in structured subsets. The code for our method is available here: https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection

</details>


### [91] [Streaming Video Generation with Streaming Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07508)
*Hanhui Wang,Yiming Xie,Haiwen Feng,Zhaoyang Lv,Shenlong Wang,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamForce是一个流式视频生成框架，通过统一的力表示和蒸馏流程，实现了对局部和全局时变力的因果响应，支持物理可控的视频生成，在单GPU上达到16.6 FPS，在力遵循和运动真实性方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型要么为不同力类型训练独立模型，要么假设力是固定的，或依赖非因果处理方式，缺乏一个能够实时且一致地响应时变力的统一框架。

Method: 设计统一的力表示作为控制信号，开发蒸馏流程用于力可控视频生成，将自回归效率与力响应能力相结合，采用因果处理方式同时支持局部和全局的时变力输入。

Result: 在单GPU上运行速度达16.6 FPS，在力遵循程度和运动真实性方面均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: StreamForce证明了通过统一力表示和因果建模，可以有效实现物理可控的流式视频生成，在保持逼真的光度与动态真实感的同时具备实时性能。

Abstract: We introduce StreamForce, a streaming video generation framework that enables physically grounded control through continuous force inputs. Unlike prior video models that train separate models for different force types, assume fixed forces, or rely on non-causal processing, StreamForce is a causal and unified model that responds instantly and coherently to both local and global, time-varying forces. To achieve this, we design a unified force representation as a control signal and develop a distillation pipeline for force-controllable video generation. Our model combines autoregressive efficiency with force responsiveness, sustaining stable photometric and dynamic realism. StreamForce runs at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both force adherence and motion realism. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/

</details>


### [92] [MemDreamer: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Hierarchical Graph Memory and Agentic Retrieval Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07512)
*Cong Chen,Guo Gan,Kaixiang Ji,ChaoYang Zhang,Zhen Yang,Guangming Yao,Hao Chen,Jingdong Chen,Yi Yuan,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemDreamer 提出了一种分层图记忆与智能体推理框架，将长视频理解解耦为感知与推理，通过分层图记忆（三层次语义抽象）和智能体工具增强检索（观察-推理-行动循环），仅用全上下文2%的token即可在四个基准上达到SOTA，与人类专家差距缩小至3.7分，并发现VLM逻辑推理能力与长视频理解呈强正线性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型处理长视频时面临token爆炸和注意力稀释问题，无法有效理解数小时长的视频内容。

Method: 提出MemDreamer框架，将感知与推理解耦：(1) 增量流式处理视频，构建层次化图记忆（三层次语义抽象架构，底层图捕获时空与因果关系）；(2) 推理阶段采用智能体工具增强检索，通过观察-推理-行动循环在层次结构中导航、搜索节点、遍历逻辑边。

Result: 四个主流基准上达到SOTA，与人类专家差距仅3.7分；推理上下文窗口仅为全上下文摄入的2%，同时获得12.5分的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: VLM的逻辑推理能力与长视频理解性能之间存在强正线性相关，智能体能力扩展为多模态理解建立了新范式。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models struggle with hours-long videos because processing full-length visual sequences induces prohibitive token explosion and attention dilution. To overcome this, we introduce MemDreamer to decouple perception and reasoning, shifting long-video understanding into an agentic exploration process. As a plug-and-play framework, it incrementally streams videos to construct a Hierarchical Graph Memory, a top-down three-tier architecture for semantic abstraction, anchored by a foundational graph capturing spatiotemporal and causal relations. During inference, the reasoning model employs agentic tool-augmented retrieval, navigating hierarchies, searching nodes, and traversing logical edges via an Observation-Reason-Action loop. Experiments show MemDreamer achieves SOTA results across four mainstream benchmarks, narrowing the gap with human experts to only 3.7 points. It constrains the reasoning context window to merely 2% of full-context ingestion while delivering a 12.5 point absolute accuracy gain. Furthermore, statistical analysis uncovers a strong positive linear correlation between an VLM's performance on logic reasoning and long-video understanding benchmarks, establishing agentic capability scaling as a new paradigm for multimodal comprehension.

</details>


### [93] [UniSHARP: Universal Sharp Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07514)
*Meixi Song,Dizhe Zhang,Hao Ren,Ruiyang Zhang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSHARP，通过将多种相机系统（从透视到全景）的图像对齐到统一的全方位潜在空间，扩展SHARP方法实现通用单目渲染。该方法在特征和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐，采用基于射线的通用表示和UniK3D启发的编解码器，在构建的多相机基准上大幅优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP方法基于针孔相机假设，无法直接推广到鱼眼、全景等非传统相机系统。本文旨在移除这一限制，实现跨不同相机系统的通用单目渲染。

Method: 1) 将不同图像对齐到统一的全方位潜在空间；2) 在特征空间和高斯空间中执行隐式对齐；3) 高斯基元沿射线和径向距离以基于射线的通用表示排列；4) 使用UniK3D启发的编码器提取2D语义和3D空间特征，联合解码生成完整高斯云。

Result: 构建了涵盖多种成像系统和场景的基准测试，并按视场角（FoV）分层评估。实验表明UniSHARP在所有设置下均大幅优于对比方法。

Conclusion: UniSHARP成功将SHARP扩展到通用相机系统，验证了统一全方位潜在空间对齐策略的有效性，为全景渲染提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on extending SHARP, the popular photorealistic view synthesis method, for universal monocular rendering across a continuum of camera systems, from conventional perspective cameras to wide-field-of-view, fisheye and omnidirectional panoramic settings. To overcome the pinhole-specific assumptions of SHARP, our key idea is to align various images in a unified omnidirectional latent space. Thus, we propose UniSHARP, which performs implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. Specifically, Gaussian primitives are arranged along rays and radial distances in a ray-based universal representation, while 2D semantic and 3D spatial features extracted from UniK3D-inspired encoders are jointly decoded to generate the complete Gaussian cloud. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we construct a benchmark covering diverse imaging systems across various scenes. The benchmark is further stratified by field of view (FoV) to enable fine-grained assessment of the universal monocular rendering task. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UniSHARP, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin. The project page can be found at: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/

</details>


### [94] [T2LM: Long-Term 3D Human Motion Generation from Multiple Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00636)
*Taeryung Lee,Fabien Baradel,Thomas Lucas,Kyoung Mu Lee,Gregory Rogez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出T2LM框架，通过1D卷积VQVAE和Transformer文本编码器，无需顺序数据即可生成长时序3D人体动作，在长短期动作生成任务中均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序动作生成方法依赖昂贵的顺序数据集，且生成的帧间动作存在不自然的间隙，亟需一种无需顺序数据即可生成平滑连续长序列动作的方法。

Method: T2LM由两部分组成：1）1D卷积VQVAE，将动作序列压缩为潜在向量序列，局部时间感受野保证训练与生成序列的时间一致性；2）基于Transformer的文本编码器，将输入文本预测为潜在序列。推理时将句子序列翻译为连续潜在向量流，经VQVAE解码器生成动作。

Result: T2LM在长时序动作生成任务上超越先前方法，且与单动作生成SOTA模型性能相当，同时克服了对顺序数据的依赖。

Conclusion: T2LM通过1D卷积VQVAE的局部时间约束，仅使用短序列训练即可实现平滑的长序列动作生成，为长时序动作生成提供了无需顺序数据的有效方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenging problem of long-term 3D human motion generation. Specifically, we aim to generate a long sequence of smoothly connected actions from a stream of multiple sentences (i.e., paragraph). Previous long-term motion generating approaches were mostly based on recurrent methods, using previously generated motion chunks as input for the next step. However, this approach has two drawbacks: 1) it relies on sequential datasets, which are expensive; 2) these methods yield unrealistic gaps between motions generated at each step. To address these issues, we introduce simple yet effective T2LM, a continuous long-term generation framework that can be trained without sequential data. T2LM comprises two components: a 1D-convolutional VQVAE, trained to compress motion to sequences of latent vectors, and a Transformer-based Text Encoder that predicts a latent sequence given an input text. At inference, a sequence of sentences is translated into a continuous stream of latent vectors. This is then decoded into a motion by the VQVAE decoder; the use of 1D convolutions with a local temporal receptive field avoids temporal inconsistencies between training and generated sequences. This simple constraint on the VQ-VAE allows it to be trained with short sequences only and produces smoother transitions. T2LM outperforms prior long-term generation models while overcoming the constraint of requiring sequential data; it is also competitive with SOTA single-action generation models.

</details>


### [95] [Image class translation: visual inspection of class-specific hypotheticals and classification based on translation distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08973)
*Mikyla K. Bowen,Jesse W. Wilson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将图像翻译网络泛化用于图像分类，通过将输入图像转换为类别特定的假设图像，并比较翻译距离来实现可解释的分类。该方法在黑色素瘤分类中达到80%准确率（使用2维特征空间），在骨髓细胞学图像上甚至超越传统CNN（92% vs 89%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN用于医学图像分类时缺乏可解释性，且对域外样本的错误决策具有高置信度，这是一个主要障碍。作者希望提出一种更可解释的替代方案。

Method: 训练图像到图像的翻译网络，将输入图像翻译为类别特定的假设图像；然后通过视觉和定量方式比较输入与翻译结果；使用翻译距离（即输入需要改变多少才能符合某一类别）作为低维特征向量进行分类，并分析其聚类和趋势。

Result: 在黑色素瘤/良性皮肤镜图像上，翻译距离分类器用2维特征空间达80%准确率（传统CNN用约62,000维特征空间达85%）；视觉检查揭示了数据集偏差（如黑色素瘤照片中更多比例尺）；在骨髓细胞学图像上，翻译距离分类器在3类和6类场景中均超越传统CNN（92% vs 89%，90% vs 86%）。

Conclusion: 该概念验证表明图像到图像翻译可超越艺术/风格变换，用于暴露数据集偏差、进行降维和数据集可视化，在某些情况下甚至优于传统端到端CNN分类器。

Abstract: Purpose: A major barrier to the implementation of artificial intelligence for medical applications is automated CNNs' lack of explainability and high confidence for incorrect decisions, specifically with out-of-domain samples. We propose a generalization of image translation networks for image classification and demonstrate translation networks' potential as a more interpretable alternative to conventional black-box classifiers.
  Approach: We train an image-to-image network to translate an input image to class-specific hypotheticals, and then compare these with the input, both visually and quantitatively. Translation distances, the degree of alteration needed to conform to one class or another, are examined for clusters and trends, and used as a simple low-dimensional feature vector for classification.
  Results: On melanoma/benign dermoscopy images, a translation distance classifier achieved 80% accuracy using only a 2-dimensional feature space (versus 85% for a conventional CNN using a ~62,000-dimensional feature space). Visual inspection of rendered images revealed dataset biases, like more scalebars in melanoma photographs than in benign lesions. Image distributions in translation distance space revealed a natural separation along the lines of dermatologist decision to biopsy, rather than between malignant and benign. On bone marrow cytology images, translation distance classifiers outperformed a conventional CNN in both 3-class (92% accuracy vs 89% for CNN) and 6-class (90% vs 86% for CNN) scenarios.
  Conclusions: This proof-of-concept shows the potential for image-to-image translation to go beyond artistic/stylistic changes and to expose dataset biases, perform dimension reduction and dataset visualization, and in some cases, potentially outperform conventional end-to-end CNN classifiers.

</details>


### [96] [MoDA: Modulation Adapter for Fine-Grained Visual Grounding in Instructional MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01850)
*Wayner Barrios,Andrés Villa,Juan León Alcázar,SouYoung Jin,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoDA是一种轻量级调制适配器模块，通过指令引导的通道级乘法调制增强多模态大语言模型的细粒度视觉定位能力，在12个基准测试上对三种不同架构（LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-MoRE、Qwen3-VL）均取得一致提升，且计算开销极小（<1% FLOPs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在细粒度视觉定位方面表现不足，根本原因在于视觉patch表示中存在语义纠缠问题——单个patch混合了多个不同的视觉元素，导致模型难以聚焦于与指令相关的细节。

Method: MoDA作为轻量级适配器模块，在已对齐的特征上通过跨注意力机制将语言指令与预对齐的视觉特征进行交互，生成动态调制掩码，以通道级乘法调制方式实现细粒度控制，无需额外监督或架构修改。

Result: 在12个基准测试上，LLaVA-1.5系列MMVP提升+12.0分；LLaVA-MoRE系列ScienceQA提升+4.8分；Qwen3-VL系列ScienceQA提升+4.9、RealWorldQA提升+4.1、GQA提升+3.8。计算开销低于1% FLOPs。

Conclusion: MoDA通过轻量级的通道级调制策略有效解决了视觉语义纠缠问题，在不同架构和编码器上均展现出良好的泛化能力，为增强MLLM细粒度视觉理解提供了一种高效且即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in instruction-following tasks by integrating pretrained visual encoders with large language models (LLMs). However, existing approaches often struggle with fine-grained visual grounding due to semantic entanglement in visual patch representations, where individual patches blend multiple distinct visual elements, making it difficult for models to focus on instruction-relevant details. To address this challenge, we propose MoDA (Modulation Adapter), a lightweight module that enhances visual grounding through instruction-guided channel-wise modulation. Unlike token-level methods such as Q-Former that perform additive feature selection, MoDA operates at the channel level through multiplicative modulation on already-aligned features, enabling fine-grained control over which embedding dimensions are relevant for each instruction. Following the standard LLaVA training protocol, MoDA applies cross-attention between language instructions and pre-aligned visual features, generating dynamic modulation masks without architectural modifications or additional supervision. We evaluate MoDA across 12 benchmarks spanning visual question answering, vision-centric reasoning, and hallucination detection, including recent 2024 benchmarks (MMVP, CV-Bench, MMStar, RealWorldQA), on three distinct MLLM architectures: LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-MoRE (2025), and Qwen3-VL (2025). MoDA delivers consistent gains across all three families, with +12.0 points on MMVP for the LLaVA-1.5 family and +4.8 points on ScienceQA for the LLaVA-MoRE family, and +4.9 ScienceQA, +4.1 RealWorldQA, and +3.8 GQA on Qwen3-VL, confirming that the gains generalize beyond CLIP-based encoders with minimal overhead (<1% FLOPs). Code is available at https://github.com/waybarrios/MoDA.

</details>


### [97] [Scalable GANs with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24935)
*Sangeek Hyun,MinKyu Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成对抗网络（GANs）的可扩展性，提出在紧凑的变分自编码器潜在空间中训练，并采用纯Transformer架构的生成器和判别器。针对缩放时出现的早期层利用不足和优化不稳定问题，提出了轻量级中间监督和宽度感知学习率调整等简单解决方案。实验表明，所提出的GAT模型在ImageNet-256上仅用60个epoch就达到了SOTA性能（FID 2.18）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展性推动了生成式建模的近期进展，但其原理在对抗学习领域尚未被充分探索。本文旨在研究GANs的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 在紧凑的VAE潜在空间中训练；2) 采用纯Transformer架构的生成器和判别器；3) 提出轻量级中间监督解决早期层利用不足问题；4) 提出宽度感知学习率调整解决优化不稳定问题。

Result: GAT-XL/2在ImageNet-256上仅用60个epoch达到FID 2.18，实现了单步、类别条件生成的最先进性能，比强基线少用4倍epoch。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer和潜在空间的GAN（GAT）能够在大范围容量（S到XL）上可靠训练，验证了所提可扩展性方案的有效性。

Abstract: Scalability has driven recent advances in generative modeling, yet its principles remain underexplored for adversarial learning. We investigate the scalability of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) through two design choices that have proven to be effective in other types of generative models: training in a compact Variational Autoencoder latent space and adopting purely transformer-based generators and discriminators. Training in latent space enables efficient computation while preserving perceptual fidelity, and this efficiency pairs naturally with plain transformers, whose performance scales with computational budget. Building on these choices, we analyze failure modes that emerge when naively scaling GANs. Specifically, we find issues as underutilization of early layers in the generator and optimization instability as the network scales. Accordingly, we provide simple and scale-friendly solutions as lightweight intermediate supervision and width-aware learning-rate adjustment. Our experiments show that GAT, a purely transformer-based and latent-space GANs, can be easily trained reliably across a wide range of capacities (S through XL). Moreover, GAT-XL/2 achieves state-of-the-art single-step, class-conditional generation performance (FID of 2.18) on ImageNet-256 in just 60 epochs, 4x fewer epochs than strong baselines. Project page: https://hse1032.github.io/GAT.

</details>


### [98] [Zero-Shot Polygon Matching with Pre-trained Models for Pose Estimation and Polygon Cloud from Challenging Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05949)
*Chang Li,Xingtao Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出零样本多边形匹配范式Z(PM)²，结合预训练模型与几何约束，实现从0D/1D基元到2D多边形的匹配扩展，在多个数据集上 outperforms 现有方法约32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体匹配在0D点和1D线基元上已成熟，但2D多边形匹配因视差 discontinuity、尺度变化、依赖训练数据及泛化能力差等问题尚未被探索，限制了姿态估计和3D重建等下游任务。

Method: 提出Z(PM)²范式，包含三个核心阶段：1）检测器利用预训练SAM模型将分割掩码向量化为图结构多边形；2）全局匹配器使用双向金字塔和多几何约束处理视角变化；3）局部匹配器利用局部-整体二分图优化解决视差 discontinuity 和拓扑不一致。此外，提出多边形匹配引导的姿态估计和多边形云概念。

Result: 在ISPRS、KITTI、ScanNet、SceneFlow、DTU五个数据集上，Z(PM)²匹配面积得分为68.60%，比MESA高约32%，在区域级姿态估计中排名第一，且无需训练即可实现强零样本泛化。

Conclusion: Z(PM)²首次将匹配从0D/1D基元扩展到2D多边形，通过零样本范式有效解决了多边形匹配的关键挑战，生成了比点云和线云更结构完整、语义丰富的3D表示。

Abstract: While stereo matching has achieved maturity for 0D point and 1D line primitives, establishing correspondences for 2D polygons remains largely unexplored due to challenges including disparity discontinuity, scale variation, training dependency, and poor generalization, limiting downstream tasks such as pose estimation and 3D reconstruction. To address these issues, we are the first to propose a Zero-shot Polygon Matching paradigm with Pre-trained Models (i.e., Z(PM)2), which combines learned features and handcrafted geometric constraints through plug-and-play modules, extending matching from 0D/1D primitives to 2D polygons. The pipeline comprises three core stages: Firstly, detector leverages the pre-trained segment anything model to vectorize segmentation masks into graph-structured polygons integrating geometry and texture; Secondly, global matcher uses bidirectional-pyramid and multi-geometric constraints to handle viewpoint variation; Thirdly, local matcher leverages local-holistic bipartite graph optimization to resolve disparity discontinuity and topological inconsistency. Moreover, we develop polygon-matching-guided pose estimation using correspondences to obtain well-distributed, low-redundancy homologous points, and pioneer the polygon cloud concept with an optimal surface generation method, producing structurally complete and semantically rich 3D representations beyond point and line clouds. Since no polygon matching methods from stereo imagery are available for direct comparison, we selected state-of-the-art (SoTA) methods close to this task as baselines. Extensive experiments on five challenging datasets (ISPRS, KITTI, ScanNet, SceneFlow, DTU) show Z(PM)2 achieves a 68.60% matching area score, outperforming MESA by approximately 32% and ranking first in area-level pose estimation, with competitive speed and strong zero-shot generalization without any training requirement.

</details>


### [99] [RISE: Single Static Radar-based Indoor Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14019)
*Kaichen Zhou,Laura Dodds,Sayed Saad Afzal,Fadel Adib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RISE 是首个基于单静态毫米波雷达的室内场景理解基准和系统，通过双角度多径增强和模拟到真实的分层扩散框架，实现了布局重建和物体检测，在保护隐私的同时大幅提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学传感器（RGB、LiDAR）在室内环境存在严重遮挡和隐私风险，而毫米波雷达虽具隐私保护与穿透能力，但其低空间分辨率导致几何推理困难。传统方法将多径反射视为噪声，未充分利用其中的丰富几何线索。

Method: 提出 Bi-Angular Multipath Enhancement（双角度多径增强），显式建模到达角（AoA）和离去角（AoD）以恢复二次（鬼影）反射从而揭示不可见结构；在此基础上，设计模拟到真实的层次化扩散框架（Hierarchical Diffusion），将碎片化雷达响应转化为完整的布局重建和物体检测。

Result: 在包含100条真实室内轨迹、50,000帧数据的大规模数据集上，RISE 在毫米波布局重建中将 Chamfer 距离降低60%（至16cm），并首次实现基于毫米波的物体检测，达到58% IoU。

Conclusion: RISE 为使用单静态雷达进行几何感知且隐私保护的室内场景理解建立了新的基础。

Abstract: Robust and privacy-preserving indoor scene understanding remains a fundamental open problem. While optical sensors such as RGB and LiDAR offer high spatial fidelity, they suffer from severe occlusions and introduce privacy risks in indoor environments. In contrast, millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar preserves privacy and penetrates obstacles, but its inherently low spatial resolution makes reliable geometric reasoning difficult. We introduce RISE, the first benchmark and system for single-static-radar indoor scene understanding, jointly targeting layout reconstruction and object detection. RISE is built upon the key insight that multipath reflections-traditionally treated as noise-encode rich geometric cues. To exploit this, we propose a Bi-Angular Multipath Enhancement that explicitly models Angle-of-Arrival and Angle-of-Departure to recover secondary (ghost) reflections and reveal invisible structures. On top of these enhanced observations, a simulation-to-reality Hierarchical Diffusion framework transforms fragmented radar responses into complete layout reconstruction and object detection. Our benchmark contains 50,000 frames collected across 100 real indoor trajectories, forming the first large-scale dataset dedicated to single, static, radar-based indoor scene understanding. Extensive experiments show that RISE reduces the Chamfer Distance by 60% (down to 16 cm) compared to the state of the art in mmWave layout reconstruction, and delivers the first mmWave-based object detection, achieving 58% IoU. These results establish RISE as a new foundation for geometry-aware and privacy-preserving indoor scene understanding using a single static radar. Our website and code are available at https://rise-cvpr.github.io.

</details>


### [100] [Take a Peek: Efficient Encoder Adaptation for Few-Shot Semantic Segmentation via LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10521)
*Pasquale De Marinis,Gennaro Vessio,Giovanna Castellano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaP方法通过基于支持集的条件轻量级特征空间偏移，利用低秩适应（LoRA）微调编码器，以最小计算开销增强编码器对未见类别的适应性，在多个FSS和跨域FSS基准上持续提升分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前FSS研究主要聚焦于改进解码器，而编码器对未见类别提取有意义特征的能力有限，这成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出Take a Peek (TaP)方法，通过引入基于支持集条件的轻量级特征空间偏移，利用低秩适应（LoRA）微调编码器，实现快速适应新类别同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在COCO 20^i、Pascal 5^i以及DeepGlobe、ISIC、Chest X-ray等跨域数据集上，TaP在多种模型和shot设置下持续提升分割性能，尤其在复杂多类场景中增益显著。低秩适应即可实现强性能，保证计算效率。

Conclusion: TaP通过解决编码器泛化到新类别的关键限制，为更鲁棒、高效和可泛化的分割系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Few-shot semantic segmentation (FSS) aims to segment novel classes in query images using only a small annotated support set. While prior research has mainly focused on improving decoders, the encoder's limited ability to extract meaningful features for unseen classes remains a key bottleneck. In this work, we introduce \textit{Take a Peek} (TaP), a simple yet effective method that enhances encoder adaptability for both FSS and cross-domain FSS \rev{by inducing a lightweight \textit{feature-space shift} conditioned on the support set}. TaP leverages Low-Rank Adaptation to fine-tune the encoder on the support set with minimal computational overhead, enabling fast adaptation to novel classes while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Our method is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing FSS pipelines. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks--including COCO $20^i$, Pascal $5^i$, and cross-domain datasets such as DeepGlobe, ISIC, and Chest X-ray--demonstrate that TaP consistently improves segmentation performance across diverse models and shot settings. Notably, TaP delivers significant gains in complex multi-class scenarios, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic settings. A rank sensitivity analysis also shows that strong performance can be achieved even with low-rank adaptations, thereby ensuring computational efficiency. By addressing a critical limitation in FSS--the encoder's generalization to novel classes--TaP paves the way toward more robust, efficient, and generalizable segmentation systems. The code is available at https://github.com/pasqualedem/TakeAPeek.

</details>


### [101] [Calibrating Uncertainty for Zero-Shot Adversarial CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12997)
*Wenjing Lu,Zerui Tao,Yuning Qiu,Dongping Zhang,Yang Yang,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对CLIP模型的对抗性微调方法，通过狄利克雷分布重参数化输出，同时优化分类准确性和不确定性校准，解决了对抗扰动下模型过度自信和校准失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性微调方法主要关注干净样本与对抗样本之间的logits匹配，忽视了不确定性校准，且对抗扰动不仅降低准确率，还会抑制不确定性导致过度自信，存在鲁棒性之外的可信度缺口。

Method: 将CLIP输出重参数化为狄利克雷分布的浓度参数，构建统一表示以捕获相对语义结构和置信度大小，实现扰动下的整体分布对齐，超越单logits锚定。

Result: 在多个零样本基准上，该方法显著改善了不确定性校准，在保持干净准确率的同时取得了具有竞争力的对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过同时考虑准确性和不确定性的对抗微调目标，能够有效弥合对抗鲁棒性与校准不确定性之间的可靠性缺口。

Abstract: CLIP delivers strong zero-shot classification but remains highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Prior adversarial fine-tuning work primarily matches predicted logits between clean and adversarial examples, which overlooks uncertainty calibration and may degrade the zero-shot generalization. A common expectation in reliable uncertainty estimation is that predictive uncertainty should increase as inputs become more difficult or shift away from the training distribution. However, we frequently observe the opposite in the adversarial setting: perturbations not only degrade accuracy but also suppress uncertainty, leading to severe miscalibration and over-confidence. This reveals a critical reliability gap beyond robustness. To bridge this gap, we propose an adversarial fine-tuning objective for CLIP considering both accuracy and uncertainty. By reparameterizing CLIP outputs as the concentration parameters of a Dirichlet distribution, we propose a unified representation that captures relative semantic structure and confidence magnitude. This enables holistic distribution alignment under perturbations, moving beyond single-logit anchoring and restoring calibrated uncertainty. Experiments across multiple zero-shot benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly improves uncertainty calibration and achieves competitive adversarial robustness while preserving clean accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Measurement-Consistent Langevin Corrector for Stabilizing Latent Diffusion Inverse Problem Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04791)
*Lee Hyoseok,Sohwi Lim,Eunju Cha,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于潜在扩散模型（LDM）的反问题求解器存在不稳定性，归因于求解器动态与稳定反向扩散动态之间的差异，并提出了一种理论驱动的即插即用稳定模块——测量一致朗之万校正器（MCLC），通过测量一致的朗之万更新来稳定求解器，优于依赖线性流形假设的先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有基于潜在扩散模型（LDM）的反问题求解器普遍存在的不稳定性问题。

Method: 首先将不稳定性归因于求解器动态与稳定反向扩散动态之间的差异，进而提出Measurement-Consistent Langevin Corrector (MCLC)，一种理论驱动的即插即用稳定模块，通过测量一致的朗之万更新来校正求解器。

Result: MCLC相比依赖线性流形假设的先前方法，在潜在空间中表现出更稳定、更可靠的行为。

Conclusion: MCLC提供了一种 principled（有理论依据的）稳定机制，有效弥补了求解器动态与扩散模型动态之间的差距，提升了LDM反问题求解器的稳定性。

Abstract: While latent diffusion models (LDMs) have emerged as powerful priors for inverse problems, existing LDM-based solvers frequently suffer from instability. In this work, we first identify the instability as a discrepancy between the solver dynamics and stable reverse diffusion dynamics learned by the diffusion model, and show that reducing this gap stabilizes the solver. Building on this, we introduce \textit{Measurement-Consistent Langevin Corrector (MCLC)}, a theoretically grounded plug-and-play stabilization module that remedies the LDM-based inverse problem solvers through measurement-consistent Langevin updates. Compared to prior approaches that rely on linear manifold assumptions, which often fail to hold in latent space, MCLC provides a principled stabilization mechanism, leading to more stable and reliable behavior in latent space.

</details>


### [103] [COMPOSE: Hypergraph Cover Optimization for Multi-view 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09698)
*Tony Danjun Wang,Tolga Birdal,Nassir Navab,Lennart Bastian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COMPOSE 提出了一种基于加权精确覆盖优化的无训练多视角3D人体姿态估计方法，通过超图建模和全局组合优化替代传统的成对关联策略，在无需3D监督的情况下显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的多视角3D人体姿态估计方法依赖成对关联来建模对应问题，全局一致性仅作为下游约束；这种局部成对匹配在遮挡和噪声检测下脆弱，局部误差会全局传播。

Method: 将多视角3D人体姿态估计重构为超图上的加权精确覆盖优化问题，用单一全局组合目标替代成对关联和后验一致性约束；引入几何剪枝策略缩减候选空间，并提出两种求解器：精确整数线性规划和基于信念传播的可扩展松弛方法。

Result: 无需任何3D监督，COMPOSE 在平均精度上比最优的优化方法提升31个百分点，比自监督学习方法提升13个百分点。

Conclusion: 高阶组合关联对无训练多视角3D人体姿态估计是有效的，能够在不依赖标注数据的情况下显著超越现有优化方法和自监督学习方法。

Abstract: 3D human pose estimation from sparse multi-view camera rigs is an essential task for numerous applications, including action recognition, sports analysis, and human-robot interaction. While learned methods dominate the field on benchmarks, they require large annotated datasets; training-free optimization-based methods remain promising as they circumvent 3D supervision by solving a correspondence problem across views from 2D detections. Existing combinatorial formulations rely on pairwise associations to model this correspondence problem and enforce global consistency across views only as a downstream constraint. However, reconciling locally plausible pairwise matches becomes brittle under occlusion and noisy detections, where local errors propagate globally. We propose COMPOSE, which recasts multi-view 3D human pose estimation as a weighted exact-cover optimization over a hypergraph of person hypotheses. Our formulation replaces pairwise association and post-hoc consistency enforcement with a single global combinatorial objective. To address the exponentially large candidate space, we introduce a geometric pruning strategy alongside two complementary solvers: an exact Integer Linear Programming formulation and a scalable relaxation via Belief Propagation. Without any 3D supervision, COMPOSE improves average precision by up to 31 points over the best optimization-based method and 13 points over self-supervised learned methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of higher-order combinatorial association for training-free multi-view 3D human pose estimation.

</details>


### [104] [Enhancing Video Representations with Spatiotemporal-Semantic Residual to Mitigate Hallucinations in Video Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22574)
*Yuansheng Gao,Jinman Zhao,Tong Zhang,Xingguo Xu,Wenbin Xing,Han Bao,Zonghui Wang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViSSRes是一种轻量级MLP风格网络的推理时干预方法，通过对比随机游走和条件互信息优化视频表示，仅需单次前向传播即可显著减少视频多模态大模型的幻觉（EventHallusion上幻觉率降低40.69%，MMVU上视频理解提升18.36%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大模型存在幻觉问题，而推理时干预方法通常基于对比解码框架，存在启发式设计带来的改进有限和推理延迟增加的问题。

Method: 提出ViSSRes方法：1) 使用对比随机游走刻画视频表示的时空一致性；2) 引入条件互信息关联视频表示与模型语义理解；3) 保持模型主干冻结，通过轻量级MLP网络学习视频表示的残差，从时空和语义一致性两个角度优化；4) 推理时仅需单次前向传播，无额外推理成本。

Result: LLaVA-NeXT-Video在EventHallusion上幻觉率降低40.69%；在MMVU上（CoT设置）视频理解提升18.36%。

Conclusion: ViSSRes通过轻量级MLP网络和单次前向传播，有效缓解了视频多模态大模型的幻觉问题，同时避免了额外推理延迟。

Abstract: Although Video Large Multimodal Models have achieved strong performance in video understanding, they still suffer from hallucination. Existing inference-time intervention methods usually modify videos under the contrastive decoding framework, but their heuristic designs bring limited improvements and increase inference latency. To address these issues, we propose ViSSRes, an inference-time intervention method that enhances video representations through a lightweight MLP-style network. Specifically, we use a contrastive random walk approach to characterize the spatiotemporal consistency of video representations, and introduce conditional mutual information to associate video representations with the model's semantic understanding. With the model backbone kept frozen, ViSSRes learns residuals for video representations and optimizes them from both spatiotemporal and semantic consistency perspectives. During inference, ViSSRes requires only a single forward pass and introduces no substantial additional inference cost. Experiments show that ViSSRes reduces the hallucination rate of LLaVA-NeXT-Video on EventHallusion by 40.69% and improves video understanding on MMVU by 18.36% under the CoT setting, demonstrating its effectiveness in mitigating hallucinations.

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking Genomic Modeling Through Optical Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02014)
*Hongxin Xiang,Pengsen Ma,Yunkang Cao,Di Yu,Haowen Chen,Xinyu Yang,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpticalDNA是一种基于视觉的基因组建模框架，将DNA渲染为结构化视觉布局，利用OCR式视觉-语言模型，在450k碱基序列上以近20倍更少的有效token超越基线模型，仅需256k可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因组基础模型采用大语言模型架构，将DNA视为一维token序列，但基因组语义具有稀疏性和非连续性，导致在低信息背景区域的计算浪费，且难以实现理解驱动的长上下文压缩。

Method: 将DNA渲染为结构化视觉布局，训练OCR能力的视觉-语言模型，包含视觉DNA编码器和文档解码器；编码器生成紧凑可重建的视觉token实现高保真压缩；通过提示条件化目标（阅读、区域定位、子序列检索、掩码跨度补全）学习布局感知的DNA表示。

Result: 在多个基因组基准测试中持续优于近期基线；在450k碱基序列上达到最佳性能，有效token减少近20倍；仅用256k可训练参数，超越激活参数多985倍的模型。

Conclusion: 视觉化基因组建模范式有效解决了传统一维序列架构与稀疏基因组语义之间的结构性错配，以极低的计算代价实现了高保真压缩和长上下文建模。

Abstract: Recent genomic foundation models largely adopt large language model architectures that treat DNA as a one-dimensional token sequence. However, exhaustive sequential reading is structurally misaligned with sparse and discontinuous genomic semantics, leading to wasted computation on low-information background and preventing understanding-driven compression for long contexts. Here, we present OpticalDNA, a vision-based framework that reframes genomic modeling as Optical Character Recognition (OCR)-style document understanding. OpticalDNA renders DNA into structured visual layouts and trains an OCR-capable vision--language model with a visual DNA encoder and a document decoder, where the encoder produces compact, reconstructible visual tokens for high-fidelity compression. Building on this representation, OpticalDNA defines prompt-conditioned objectives over core genomic primitives-reading, region grounding, subsequence retrieval, and masked span completion-thereby learning layout-aware DNA representations that retain fine-grained genomic information under a reduced effective token budget. Across diverse genomic benchmarks, OpticalDNA consistently outperforms recent baselines; on sequences up to 450k bases, it achieves the best overall performance with nearly 20$\times$ fewer effective tokens, and surpasses models with up to 985$\times$ more activated parameters while tuning only 256k trainable parameters.

</details>


### [106] [The Geometry of Representational Failures in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07025)
*Daniele Savietto,Declan Campbell,André Panisson,Marco Nurisso,Giovanni Petri,Jonathan D. Cohen,Alan Perotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过分析开放权重视觉语言模型（Qwen、InternVL、Gemma）中"概念向量"的表示几何结构，发现概念向量之间的几何重叠与特定错误模式强相关，从而为模型在多目标视觉任务中的失败（如幻觉、绑定错误）提供了机制性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多目标视觉任务中表现出令人费解的失败（如幻觉、无法识别最相似物体），这些错误类似于人类认知中的"绑定问题"，但其内部机制尚不清楚。

Method: 分析开放权重VLM（Qwen、InternVL、Gemma）的表示几何结构，通过提炼"概念向量"（编码视觉概念的潜在方向），并利用引导干预（steering interventions）验证这些向量的有效性，进而研究向量间几何重叠与错误模式的关系。

Result: 验证了概念向量的有效性（可通过引导干预可靠地操纵模型行为，如将红色花强制感知为蓝色）；发现概念向量之间的几何重叠与特定错误模式（如幻觉、绑定失败）之间存在强相关。

Conclusion: 提供了理解内部表示如何塑造模型行为并驱动视觉失败的定量框架，揭示了VLM在多目标任务中失败的机制性根源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit puzzling failures in multi-object visual tasks, such as hallucinating non-existent elements or failing to identify the most similar objects among distractions. While these errors mirror human cognitive constraints, such as the 'Binding Problem', the internal mechanisms driving them in artificial systems remain poorly understood. Here, we propose a mechanistic insight by analyzing the representational geometry of open-weight VLMs (Qwen, InternVL, Gemma), comparing methodologies to distill "concept vectors'' - latent directions encoding visual concepts. We validate our concept vectors via steering interventions that reliably manipulate model behavior in both simplified and naturalistic vision tasks (e.g., forcing the model to perceive a red flower as blue). We observe that the geometric overlap between these vectors strongly correlates with specific error patterns, offering a grounded quantitative framework to understand how internal representations shape model behavior and drive visual failures.

</details>


### [107] [Modality Gap-Driven Subspace Alignment Training Paradigm For Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07026)
*Xiaomin Yu,Yi Xin,Yuhui Zhang,Wenjie Zhang,Chonghan Liu,Hanzhen Zhao,Chen Liu,Xiaoxing Hu,Ziyue Qiao,Hao Tang,Xiaobin Hu,Chengwei Qin,Hui Xiong,Yu Qiao,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出固定框架模态差距理论，将模态差距分解为稳定偏差和各向异性残差；基于此提出无需训练的ReAlign对齐方法，通过海量无配对数据的统计信息将文本表示对齐到图像表示分布；进一步提出ReVision可扩展训练范式，在预训练阶段利用对齐后的无配对文本替代昂贵的图文对，实现MLLMs的高效扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对比学习存在模态差距（Modality Gap）这一几何异常现象，即表达相同语义的不同模态嵌入位于系统偏移区域。现有方法受限于过于简化的各向同性假设，难以在大规模场景中应用。

Method: 1) 提出Fixed-frame Modality Gap Theory，在冻结参考框架内将模态差距分解为稳定偏差（stable biases）和各向异性残差（anisotropic residuals）；2) 提出ReAlign，一种无需训练的对齐策略，通过Anchor、Trace、Centroid Alignment三步，利用海量无配对数据的统计信息将文本表示对齐到图像表示分布；3) 提出ReVision，将ReAlign集成到MLLMs预训练阶段，使模型从无配对文本学习视觉表示分布。

Result: 统计对齐的无配对数据可以有效替代昂贵的高质量图文对，为MLLMs的高效扩展提供鲁棒路径。

Conclusion: 精确建模模态差距的几何形状并利用统计对齐方法，可以在无需大规模高质量图文对的情况下高效扩展多模态大语言模型。

Abstract: Despite the success of multimodal contrastive learning in aligning visual and linguistic representations, a persistent geometric anomaly, the Modality Gap, remains: embeddings of distinct modalities expressing identical semantics occupy systematically offset regions. Prior approaches to bridge this gap are largely limited by oversimplified isotropic assumptions, hindering their application in large-scale scenarios. In this paper, we address these limitations by precisely characterizing the geometric shape of the modality gap and leveraging it for efficient model scaling. First, we propose the Fixed-frame Modality Gap Theory, which decomposes the modality gap within a frozen reference frame into stable biases and anisotropic residuals. Guided by this precise modeling, we introduce ReAlign, a training-free modality alignment strategy. Utilizing statistics from massive unpaired data, ReAlign aligns text representation into the image representation distribution via a three-step process comprising Anchor, Trace, and Centroid Alignment, thereby explicitly rectifying geometric misalignment. Building on ReAlign, we propose ReVision, a scalable training paradigm for Multimodal Large Language Models~(MLLMs). ReVision integrates ReAlign into the pretraining stage, enabling the model to learn the distribution of visual representations from unpaired text before visual instruction tuning, without the need for large-scale, high-quality image-text pairs. Our framework demonstrates that statistically aligned unpaired data can effectively substitute for expensive image-text pairs, offering a robust path for the efficient scaling of MLLMs.

</details>


### [108] [Consistency-Preserving Diverse Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15287)
*Xinshuang Liu,Runfa Blark Li,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向流匹配视频生成模型的联合采样框架，在保持时序一致性的同时提升批次内多样性，通过轻量级潜空间模型避免视频解码和反向传播开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成成本高昂，每段提示通常仅生成少量样本，因此最大化每个批次的交叉视频多样性至关重要。现有图像多样性方法应用于视频时会破坏时序一致性，且需要昂贵的视频解码器反向传播。

Method: 提出联合采样框架：先应用多样性驱动更新，再移除会降低时序一致性目标的分量；使用轻量级潜空间模型计算多样性和时序一致性目标，避免视频解码和反向传播。

Result: 在流匹配文本到视频模型上实验表明，该方法在接近强联合采样基线的多样性水平上，显著提升了时序一致性和色彩自然度。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不引入额外计算负担的前提下，有效平衡了视频批次多样性与时序一致性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-video generation is expensive, so only a few samples are typically produced per prompt. In this low-sample regime, maximizing the value of each batch requires high cross-video diversity. Recent methods improve diversity for image generation, but for videos they often degrade within-video temporal consistency and require costly backpropagation through a video decoder. We propose a joint-sampling framework for flow-matching video generators that improves batch diversity while preserving temporal consistency. Our approach applies diversity-driven updates and then removes only the components that would decrease a temporal-consistency objective. To avoid image-space gradients, we compute both objectives with lightweight latent-space models, avoiding video decoding and decoder backpropagation. Experiments on a state-of-the-art text-to-video flow-matching model show diversity close to strong joint-sampling baselines while substantially improving temporal consistency and color naturalness. Our code is available at https://github.com/XinshuangL/Diverse-Video.

</details>


### [109] [SegMoTE: Token-Level Mixture of Experts for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19213)
*Yujie Lu,Jingwen Li,Sibo Ju,Yanzhou Su,he yao,Yisong Liu,Min Zhu,Junlong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegMoTE提出了一种高效自适应的医学图像分割框架，仅用不到现有大规模数据集1%的数据（MedSeg-HQ）训练，通过少量可学习参数实现跨模态和任务的动态适应，并设计了渐进式提示标记化机制支持全自动分割，在多种医学影像模态和解剖任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用交互式分割模型（如SAM）迁移到医学影像领域存在两个关键瓶颈：(i) 缺乏针对模态和解剖任务的自适应机制，导致在医学域外场景泛化能力有限；(ii) 当前医学适应方法在大规模异质数据集上不加选择地微调，导致噪声监督、高成本和负迁移。

Method: 提出SegMoTE框架，保留SAM的原始提示接口、高效推理和零样本泛化能力，仅引入少量可学习参数实现跨模态和任务的动态适应；设计渐进式提示标记化机制，实现全自动分割，减少对标注的依赖；在精心筛选的MedSeg-HQ数据集（<现有大规模数据集1%）上训练。

Result: 在多种成像模态和解剖任务上达到SOTA性能，验证了在极低标注成本下将通用分割模型高效、鲁棒、可扩展地适应到医学领域的效果。

Conclusion: SegMoTE是首个在极低标注成本下实现通用分割模型高效、鲁棒、可扩展适应到医学领域的工作，推动了基础视觉模型在临床应用中的实际部署。
