In addition, we design a prompt-based semantic fusion module to integrate representations across multiple views for downstream prediction. Extensive experiments show that CHoE consistently improves performance in few-shot cross-domain applications, outperforming all baseline approaches.

</details>


### [367] [Leveraging Error Diversity in Group Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17333)
*Wenpu Liu,Yuqi Xu,Weichu Xie,Yongfu Zhu,Shuai Dong,Ziyue Wang,Wenqi Shao,Xiaoying Zhang,Tong Yang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EDAS，通过利用组内错误多样性信号来调制强化学习中的优势函数，从而提升RLVR训练效果，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在采样多个响应后仅基于个体正确性分配二元奖励，忽略了组输出中的集体结构（特别是错误分布）。实证分析发现错误多样性是训练成功的有力预测指标，因此作者希望利用这一被忽视的信息来改进RLVR。

Method: 提出EDAS（Error Diversity Advantage Shaping），一种轻量级、算法无关的后处理技术。它根据组内错误多样性调制错误rollout的优势信号：放大对占主导地位的重复错误的惩罚，减弱对稀有探索性错误的惩罚，从而鼓励模型维持多样化推理路径并抑制错误固着。EDAS可作为后置调整无缝集成到任何RLVR算法中。

Result: 在多个主流RLVR方法、多种模型和七个数学基准上验证，EDAS带来一致改进。其中在Qwen3-8B上对DAPO平均提升6.29个点。

Conclusion: 利用组rollout中的潜在信息是增强RLVR的广泛有效策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) typically samples multiple responses per prompt and assigns binary rewards based on individual correctness, yet the collective structure of the group output, specifically the distribution of errors, is largely discarded. We identify this as a missed opportunity: empirical analysis reveals that error diversity within a group is a strong predictor of training success, with problems eliciting diverse wrong answers benefiting substantially more from RLVR than those producing homogeneous failures. Motivated by this observation, we propose Error Diversity Advantage Shaping (EDAS), a lightweight, algorithm-agnostic technique that modulates the advantage signal for incorrect rollouts based on intra-group error diversity. EDAS amplifies penalties for dominant, repeated errors and attenuates penalties for rare, exploratory ones, thereby encouraging the model to maintain diverse reasoning paths and discouraging error perseveration. Crucially, EDAS operates as a simple post-hoc adjustment that can be seamlessly integrated into any RLVR algorithm. We validate EDAS on top of several mainstream RLVR methods across a series of models and seven challenging math benchmarks, demonstrating consistent improvements. Notably, EDAS yields an average improvement of 6.29 points over DAPO on Qwen3-8B across seven benchmarks, confirming that exploiting the latent information in group rollouts is a broadly effective strategy for strengthening RLVR.

</details>


### [368] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量的后验分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型是否真正利用了领域相关结构而非分布偏差。在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中，该框架揭示了预测性能相似的模型具有截然不同的分布响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中表现优异，但其强基准性能常被错误解释为具备科学意义的行为，模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差进行预测，这些特征在留出数据上有效但未对齐领域相关结构。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，核心是Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）。给定训练好的黑盒预测器和编码领域知识的外部结构先验，在结构引导的输入扰动下评估模型输出，通过三种互补度量衡量输出分布相干性：基于分位数的度量（QBM）评估位置级相干性、WCM评估序数相干性、平移不变WCM变体评估形状相干性。

Result: 在Davis激酶基准上对三个序列型DTI模型（DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB）进行实例化，发现尽管模型处于可比预测性能区间，I-SAFE揭示了它们截然不同的分布响应特征，这种差异是准确率评估所不可见的。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可结构化分解且具备外部先验的领域，为科学AI模型提供了超越准确率评估的分布级审计手段。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [369] [Scale When Needed: Adaptive Neuron-level Mixed Precision Quantization Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25054)
*Ayush K. Varshney,Konstantinos Vandikas,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Adam Orucu,Aneta Vulgarakis Feljan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经元级混合精度QAT（NMP-QAT），让每个神经元独立学习离散精度，在6G边缘设备上实现更优的压缩-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度QAT方法通常以层或通道为粒度，依赖启发式或搜索策略，忽略了神经元级别的细粒度差异，导致在资源受限的6G边缘设备上压缩效率不足。

Method: 提出NMP-QAT：每个神经元独立学习离散精度；从低比特开始，通过可微代理和直通估计器在训练信号需要时扩展位宽；保持完全离散的推理图；同时适用于权重和激活。

Result: 在电信和非电信数据集上，基于MLP和表格型基础模型架构，NMP-QAT在压缩-准确率权衡上优于混合精度QAT基线。

Conclusion: NMP-QAT适合6G网络边缘的绿色AI部署，能在资源受限设备上实现高效压缩。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained 6G edge devices demands aggressive compression with minimal accuracy loss. Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) has emerged as a leading compression approach; however, existing mixed-precision methods typically operate at coarse layer- or channel-level granularity. These methods often rely on heuristic or search-based bit-allocation strategies, which may overlook fine-grained variability at the neuron level. We propose Neuron-Level Mixed-Precision QAT (NMP-QAT), where each neuron independently learns its own discrete precision during training. Starting from low-bit precision, NMP-QAT expands bit-width only when training signals demand it, via differentiable surrogates and straight-through estimators, while preserving a fully discrete inference graph. This adaptability extends to both weights and activations, reducing memory movement. Evaluated on telecom and non-telecom datasets across MLP and tabular foundation model architectures, NMP-QAT achieves superior compression-accuracy trade-offs over mixed-precision QAT baselines, making it well-suited for Green AI deployments at the network edge.

</details>


### [370] [Autoregression-Free Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25413)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Haoyu Bian,Libin Cai,Yi Lu,Chaoning Zhang,Wei Dong,Yuanfang Guo,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归无关神经算子（AFNO），将PDE的时间演化映射到潜空间并通过流匹配学习连续时间向量场，避免自回归 rollout，在六种PDE上的实验证明其改善了长期预测稳定性并减少了误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经算子的时变PDE求解方法通常在高维物理场空间中通过自回归 rollout 进行长期预测，导致误差逐步累积；同时缺乏连续时间建模能力，难以应对长时间跨度的预测需求。

Method: 将PDE的时间演化映射到潜空间，利用流匹配（flow matching）在潜空间中学习连续时间向量场，避免显式自回归 rollout，并通过物理参数的条件化显式建模不同参数配置下的动力学。

Result: 在六种PDE上的理论分析和大量实验表明，AFNO在长期预测稳定性上优于基线方法，并且持续降低了 rollout 误差。

Conclusion: AFNO通过潜空间连续建模和流匹配有效解决了自回归 rollout 的误差累积问题，为时变PDE的长期预测提供了一种更稳定、低误差的替代方案。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from function-dependent inputs to solutions, providing an effective framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent PDEs, existing methods typically perform long-horizon prediction through autoregressive rollout directly in high-dimensional physical field spaces, where each predicted state is recursively fed back as the input for the next step. Although effective for short-term prediction, this autoregressive rollout and the lack of continuous-time modeling lead to progressive error accumulation over long-horizon rollouts. In this work, we propose Autoregression-Free Neural Operators (AFNO), which map the time evolution of PDEs into a latent space and model continuous-time vector fields within it. AFNO uses flow matching to learn the latent vector field, thereby enabling continuous evolution over extended horizons, avoiding autoregressive rollout and capturing dynamics under varying parameter configurations through explicit conditioning on physical parameters. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments on six PDEs demonstrate that AFNO improves long-horizon prediction stability and consistently reduces rollout errors compared with the baselines.

</details>


### [371] [BigMac: Breaking the Pareto Frontier of Compute and Memory in Multimodal LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25451)
*Zili Zhang,Chengxu Yang,Shenglong Zhang,Chenyu Wang,Yufan Zhang,Tuo Dai,Zhouyang Li,Yuhong Ge,Chao Jin,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BigMac提出了一种嵌套流水线训练方案，通过将编码器和生成器的计算嵌套到原始LLM流水线中，将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，同时保持LLM部分的激活内存不变，从而在MLLM训练中同时优化计算效率和内存使用，打破了计算与内存之间的帕累托边界。实验表明，BigMac相比基线系统实现了1.08×-1.9×的训练加速，且内存使用随batch size增长保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM训练系统受限于计算效率与内存效率之间的帕累托边界，改进一方必然牺牲另一方，无法同时优化。

Method: 提出BigMac，核心思想是将编码器和生成器的计算嵌套到原始LLM流水线中，形成依赖安全的嵌套流水线结构，从而将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，同时保持LLM的激活内存复杂度不变，实现与无限内存理想设定相同的计算效率。

Result: 在多种MLLM和训练负载上，BigMac实现1.08×-1.9×的训练加速比，且内存使用随batch size增加保持稳定。

Conclusion: BigMac通过嵌套流水线设计打破了计算效率与内存使用之间的帕累托边界，实现了MLLM训练中计算和内存的同步优化。

Abstract: Training multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is challenged by both model and data heterogeneity. Existing systems redesign the training pipeline to address these challenges, but remain bound by a Pareto frontier between compute and memory efficiency, improving one only at the expense of the other. We present BigMac, a new training pipeline for multimodal LLMs. The core idea of BigMac is to elegantly nest the encoder and generator computation into the original LLM pipeline, forming a dependency-safe nested pipeline structure. With this design, BigMac reduces the activation memory complexity of the encoder and generator to O(1) while keeping the activation memory complexity of the LLM unchanged. At the same time, it achieves the same computational efficiency as the idealized setting with unlimited memory. As a result, BigMac breaks the Pareto frontier between computational efficiency and memory usage, enabling simultaneous optimization of both computation and memory in MLLM training. We evaluate BigMac on multiple MLLMs and training workloads. Experimental results show that BigMac achieves a 1.08$\times$-1.9$\times$ training speedup over baseline systems while maintaining stable memory usage as batch size increases.

</details>


### [372] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒方法，仅通过模型生成的文本输出（无需访问架构或权重），利用模型对公共文本的记忆程度与参数量的关联，推断大语言模型参数规模的下界。通过构建准确率分布向量，结合成对统计检验和基于PCA的标度律估计器，在开源模型上验证有效，并成功应用于闭源模型，揭示了不同开发商的参数规模策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流大语言模型的参数量常被开发者隐瞒，而参数规模是理解模型能力和成本的关键参考指标。现有方法往往需要访问模型内部结构或权重，缺乏仅通过文本输出来推断参数量级的可行手段。

Method: 基于关键观察：广泛流传的经典文本几乎存在于所有大规模预训练语料中，模型对这类文本跨片段长度的下一词预测准确率反映了其记忆程度，而记忆能力受参数量限制。具体方法：(1) 对多样化的文本语料和片段长度聚合记忆信号，为每个模型构建准确率分布向量；(2) 基于该向量构建两种互补推断方法：成对统计检验（判断两模型谁更大）和基于PCA的标度律估计器（提取一维隐变量映射到参数量）。

Result: 在广泛的开源模型上验证，两种方法均能产生准确可靠的参数量下界。应用于流行闭源模型时，恢复了内部产品层级结构，并揭示了行业缩放策略的明显分化：部分开发者模型参数量显著增长，另一些则受限于严格的参数上限。

Conclusion: 即使仅通过API限制下的文本输出，也能系统性地探知隐藏的模型设计选择，证明黑盒条件下推断模型规模是可行的。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [373] [Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119)
*Thalea Schlender,Peter A. N. Bosman,Tanja Alderliesten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了使用遗传规划多目标进化方法来优化生存树模型，通过进化特征构建和联合优化树结构与非线性的分裂逻辑，在保持浅层树可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存树模型虽然可解释性强，但为捕捉复杂关系往往需要生长得很大，这损害了可解释性；同时，贪心构建方法可能忽略全局最优的分裂组合，限制了预测性能。因此需要在保持浅层树结构的同时提升其表达能力。

Method: 使用遗传规划进行多目标进化：(1) 进化构建具有表达力的高阶特征组合，与不同树归纳策略结合；(2) 提出联合进化方法，同时优化生存树结构和非线性的分裂逻辑。

Result: 在两个真实数据集和两种不同生存树深度下，进化特征构建在不同的树归纳策略上均提升了预测性能。

Conclusion: 多目标进化整棵树的方法因其速度快和灵活的呈现方式，最有前途。

Abstract: Survival analysis concerns the task of predicting the time until an event occurs. Often used in the medical field, survival analysis deals with incomplete (i.e., censored) data, for instance, from patients who did not experience the event during the duration of the study. For practical use, both accuracy and interpretability are important.
  Survival trees are easy-to-follow survival models that split the patient cohort recursively into discrete patient groups. Whilst survival trees can capture complex relationships, they typically need to grow large, threatening interpretability. Moreover, survival trees are often built using greedy approaches that may overlook globally optimal split combinations, limiting predictive performance.
  Shallow survival trees require expressive, higher-order feature combinations to achieve competitive accuracy. We therefore use genetic programming to multi-objectively evolve inherently inspectable feature sets and study how they interact with different tree induction strategies. We further introduce an evolutionary approach that jointly optimises the survival tree structure and the non-linear split logic.
  Our findings demonstrate that evolutionary feature construction improves predictive performance across different tree induction strategies on two real-world datasets and two different survival tree depths. Given its speed and flexible presentation, the multi-objective evolution of entire trees likely holds the most future promise.

</details>


### [374] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过四种改进（策略内展开训练、改进对话数据集、增加输入层、优化注入公式）来提升激活预言机（AO）质量，并开源了首个全面评估套件AObench，为可扩展端到端可解释性奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活预言机（AO）存在幻觉和模糊性等问题，且文本反转混淆效应使得评估困难，亟需改进训练方案和建立标准化评估。

Method: 从四个方面改进AO训练：1) 采用策略内展开（on-policy rollouts）训练；2) 改进对话数据集；3) 增加更多层作为输入；4) 优化注入公式。

Result: 能力提升幅度较小，但生活质量（质量易用性）方面的改进相当显著；并构建了首个综合评估套件AObench。

Conclusion: 该工作为改进AO及其他模型在可扩展端到端可解释性范式中奠定了基础。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [375] [Perplexity Can Miss SAE Feature Damage Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量化模型在困惑度等行为指标上可能与全精度模型相当，但其SAE特征会显著退化。INT7在Gemma-2-2B上困惑度改善的同时导致18.7%的活跃特征退化；INT6下仅51.3%的特征存活。特征存活率可从全精度特征统计量高精度预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），且RTN量化与幅度剪枝损伤的特征集高度重叠（Jaccard 0.79-0.86）。这表明行为指标不足以保证可解释性发现向量化模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型通常仅用困惑度或下游准确率评估是否可接受，但这些行为指标可能掩盖模型内部特征（如SAE特征）的损伤，导致全精度模型的可解释性发现无法可靠迁移到量化模型上。

Method: 使用冻结的SAE作为固定测量基，对相同token编码全精度和RTN量化激活，通过Pearson相关系数测量逐特征存活率，在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B上从INT8到INT4进行实验。

Result: INT7困惑度改善但18.7%特征退化；INT6下Pythia 62.4%和Gemma 51.3%特征存活，非存活特征多为模糊而非完全损坏；特征存活率可从全精度特征统计量高精度预测（AUC 0.92-0.97）；RTN量化与幅度剪枝的特征损伤集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 行为指标不足以证明全精度模型的可解释性发现能迁移到量化模型，需要特征级别的压缩审计。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and quantized models are typically judged acceptable when perplexity or downstream accuracy remains close to the full-precision original. But behavioral parity need not imply feature fidelity: the sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features used to interpret a full-precision model may change after weight rounding. We test this directly by using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, encoding full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens, and measuring per-feature survival by Pearson correlation across bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. Our central finding is that perplexity can miss feature damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7% of active SAE features, and under sliding-window evaluation INT6 also improves perplexity while only 51.3% of active features survive. Feature survival is graded rather than cliff-like, with 62.4% of active Pythia features and 51.3% of active Gemma features surviving at INT6; most non-surviving features are blurred rather than fully damaged. Survival is also predictable from full-precision feature statistics alone, with cross-validated AUC 0.92--0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Finally, RTN quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap 0.79--0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation 0.98. These results show that behavioral metrics alone are insufficient evidence that full-precision interpretability findings transfer to quantized models, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [376] [Local Guidance, Global Impact: Gaussian-Reshaped Trust Region Unlocks Behavior Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03382)
*Bingxu Liu,Jiashun Liu,Johan Obando-Ceron,Hao Wang,Runze Liu,Pablo Samuel Castro,Aaron Courville,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯信任域策略优化(GTR)，通过高斯核重塑信任域，解决PPO在非平稳环境下因缺乏几何感知导致的方向低效更新问题，在游戏、机器人控制、开放世界探索和语言模型后训练等任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PPO在非平稳/持续学习环境中表现不佳，其失败原因并非模型容量不足或裁剪过于严格，而是由于缺乏几何感知引导，导致持续进行方向低效的局部更新，阻碍策略向新行为模式的有效过渡。现有基于散度的正则化方法虽提供部分几何感知，但其单调递增的惩罚隐式地抑制了必要的大幅策略偏离。

Method: 提出高斯信任域策略优化(GTR)：1) 使用高斯核重塑信任域，产生有界且非单调的约束，在提供强局部稳定性的同时允许高优势更新下逐步放松约束；2) 引入混合高斯锚点(Mixture Gaussian Anchor)，自适应跟踪最近的策略轨迹，减少由陈旧参考导致的方差。

Result: GTR是架构无关的方法，在多种任务上取得优异表现，包括游戏、模拟机器人控制、开放世界探索和语言模型后训练。

Conclusion: 几何感知的信任域设计是应对复杂非平稳环境中鲁棒强化学习的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: While Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) demonstrates strong performance in stationary settings, we show that its standard optimization paradigm struggles in continual and non-stationary environments. The failure does not stem from insufficient model capacity or overly restrictive clipping. Instead, PPO performs persistent, directionally inefficient local updates, which indicates a lack of geometry-aware guidance for accumulating meaningful behavioral change and ultimately hindering transitions toward new behavior patterns. Although divergence-based regularization introduces partial geometric awareness, its monotonically increasing penalties implicitly discourage large policy deviations, even when such shifts are necessary for effective adaptation. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Trust Region Policy Optimization (GTR), which reshapes the trust region using a Gaussian kernel. The resulting constraint is bounded and non-monotonic, providing strong local stability while progressively relaxing under sustained high-advantage updates. To further improve robustness, we introduce a Mixture Gaussian Anchor that adapts to recent policy trajectories, reducing variance induced by stale references. GTR is architecture-agnostic and achieves strong performance across games, simulated robotic control, open-world exploration, and language model post-training. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware trust-region design can be a promising direction for robust reinforcement learning in complex non-stationary environments. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GTR_demo/README.md.

</details>


### [377] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于高斯RBF核的支持向量回归（RBF-SVR）模型的非凸优化问题，本文提出一种利用DC规划算法（DCA）的框架，通过显式构造DC分解，推导出强凸性下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，发现两者共享主导项Cαρ。实验表明Cαρ是表征DCA收敛性和初始点依赖性的核心标量，且可分解为C→Cα和γ→ρ两条独立路径，从而可在训练前后通过单一标量Cαρ预先评估DCA的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RBF-SVR模型预测函数作为目标函数的非凸优化问题，现有方法缺乏对DCA收敛性能的定量先验评估指标，需要从理论上揭示影响收敛性的关键参数及其解析关系。

Method: 利用RBF核函数的解析结构显式构造DC分解，推导出强凸性参数下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式解；通过六种基准函数的数值实验验证Cαρ作为核心标量的有效性，并分析其参数依赖路径。

Result: μ和L均由训练后对偶系数和Cα、RBF核参数γ及DC分解参数ρ共同决定，共享主导项Cαρ；数值实验表明Cαρ是表征DCA收敛性和初始点依赖性的主要标量，且可分解为C→Cα和γ→ρ两条独立路径，主要变化由SVR超参数(C,γ)控制。

Conclusion: 通过单一标量Cαρ可在训练前根据(C,γ)近似评估、训练后通过闭式精确评估DCA在RBF-SVR上的收敛性能，为实际应用提供了便捷的先验判断工具。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [378] [Spectral Scaling Laws of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04058)
*Gagik Magakyan,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Muon优化器中牛顿-舒尔茨(NS)迭代对动量矩阵奇异值谱的近似正交归一化效果随模型规模的变化规律，发现不同层级的奇异值稳定值遵循幂律分布，浅层到中深层对模型规模增长不敏感，但部分深层会随规模急剧下降，导致标准NS配置失效，据此提出了分层自适应NS迭代配置策略以节省计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器使用NS迭代近似正交归一化动量矩阵，但NS仅对较大奇异值方向有效，小奇异值方向可能未能被正确正交归一化。已有研究未涉及动量矩阵奇异值谱在训练过程中的演化规律，也不清楚这种规律如何随模型规模变化。理解这一行为对于确保大规模模型训练中更新质量至关重要。

Method: 在77M到2.8B参数范围内多个规模的语言模型中，追踪不同层级动量缓冲区的奇异值分位数，分析其稳定值随模型规模的变化规律，拟合幂律关系，并考察不同深度层级的行为差异。

Result: 1）经历短时间预热后，各层奇异值分位数稳定在由层类型和模型规模决定的常数值；2）这些稳定值随模型规模呈现清晰的幂律关系，不同层级的指数不同；3）浅层至中深层指数约为-0.25，对规模增长不敏感；4）部分深层指数可达-0.96，会随规模急剧下降，导致标准5步NS配置在更大规模下失效。

Conclusion: 基于所发现的幂律规律，可以为不同规模模型中的不同层级选择最小所需的NS迭代次数或更优系数，在保证关键方向正交归一化的前提下避免不必要的计算开销，实现计算效率与更新质量之间的平衡。

Abstract: Orthonormalized update rules have rapidly become a leading choice of optimizer for training large language models, with recent open-source state-of-the-art models adopting Muon. To keep these updates tractable, Muon performs the orthonormalization with the Newton--Schulz (NS) iteration. Since NS is only approximate, directions with small singular values fail to be orthonormalized. In Muon, NS is applied to the momentum matrix at every step, yet little is known about how the singular value spectrum of these momentum matrices behaves during training, or how that behavior changes with model size. We present the first systematic study of this question. Tracking singular value quantiles of the momentum buffer across layers in models ranging from 77M to 2.8B parameters, we observe a consistent picture: after a short burn-in, the quantiles stabilize at a value determined by the layer type and model size. These stabilization values follow remarkably clean power laws in model size, with layer-dependent exponents. Layers up to mid-late depth scale very mildly with model size $M$ (around $M^{-0.25}$), so the standard 5-step NS configuration used at academic scale will continue to orthonormalize them at much larger scales. Some of the late layers, however, scale much more aggressively (up to $M^{-0.96}$) and will fall into the NS failure regime at frontier scale unless one uses more NS iterations or better-tuned coefficients. NS iterations are computationally expensive at scale; our laws give practitioners a principled, layer-aware recipe for choosing the minimum NS configuration that still orthonormalizes the directions that matter -- avoiding unnecessary computation without sacrificing update quality.

</details>


### [379] [Scenario Generation for Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Probably Approximately Safe Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04812)
*Mohit Prashant,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）和概率屏障证书的双优化方法，通过上下界屏障证书的构建与优化，为深度强化学习策略在状态转移扰动下提供更紧的安全概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略在真实世界部署时，容易因状态转移扰动导致未知或不安全行为，而现有基于概率屏障证书的策略验证方法在存在转移不确定性或扰动时，难以获得紧致的违反安全约束概率上下界。

Method: 利用变分自编码器（VAE）近似状态空间分布，基于状态的潜在特征构建上界和下界屏障证书，将其框架化为一个对偶优化问题；在训练过程中采样位于上下界证书集差（即非鲁棒区域）的状态，以收紧概率界限。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够提供更紧的上下界，从而给出更尖锐的安全概率保证。

Conclusion: 通过VAE和双优化框架，在转移不确定性下有效收紧安全概率界限，提高了策略验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Guaranteeing safety is critical to the deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) agents in the real-world, especially as policies learned using deep RL may demonstrate susceptibility to transition perturbations that result in unknown or unsafe behaviour. A method of policy verification is to construct probabilistic barrier-certificates by sampling policy trajectories with respect to safety constraints, thereby demarcating known safe behaviour from unknown behaviour. Obtaining tight upper and lower bounds on the probability of violation of these constraints may be difficult if the policy is susceptible to transition uncertainty or perturbation that places the agent in insufficiently explored states. To address this, we approximate the distribution of the encountered state-space using a variational autoencoder (VAE) and construct upper and lower-bound barrier-certificates using latent characteristics of states to optimize for regions of known, safe behaviour with high confidence. We frame this in our work as a dual optimization problem where the lower-bound barrier-certificate presents a more conservative estimate of the safe region than the upper-bound barrier-certificate. Sampling states that lie within the set difference of the two during training, i.e. the non-robust region, allows us to tighten the upper and lower bounds to provide sharper probabilistic guarantees on safety. Within our study, we describe the guarantees placed and demonstrate the tightness of our bounds experimentally.

</details>


### [380] [Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05152)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistIL方法，利用分布性DAgger算法实现前向交叉熵优化，相较于传统RLVR和基于自蒸馏的强化学习方法，在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解等多个领域取得更好效果，并证明其具有单调策略改进和后悔界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）的方法过于狭窄，仅用单比特信号表示最终答案是否正确，忽略了执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正和模型自评估等丰富反馈信息。需探索如何有效利用这些丰富的反馈信号来更好地训练推理模型。

Method: 提出DistIL方法，使用分布性DAgger算法变体，让学习器能够局部访问当前策略所访问状态上的专家分布。通过简单的前向交叉熵目标函数进行优化，该目标接受黑盒专家，其序列级梯度通过将未来的专家-学生分歧传播回早期决策来实现丰富的信用分配。

Result: 在科学推理、编程和解决困难数学问题等多个领域，DistIL方法优于传统的RLVR和基于自蒸馏（反向KL或Jensen-Shannon散度）的强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于反向KL或Jensen-Shannon散度的自蒸馏目标无法保证单调策略改进，即使专家具有更高奖励，其更新也可能增加较差动作的概率。而前向交叉熵目标保证单调策略改进并具有后悔界保证，同时优化教师加权成功可能性的下界，从而提升Pass@N指标。

Abstract: Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.

</details>


### [381] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示关系学习中传统扁平排行榜评估方法因忽略数据集内在几何结构而引入系统性偏差，提出基于曲率分层的评估框架，并在14个数据集上对18个模型进行评测，发现模型性能高度依赖于数据几何特性，进一步提出几何感知评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系学习评估方法依赖跨异质数据集平均性能的扁平排行榜，隐含地假设了统一的底层结构，但该假设会引入系统性偏差：掩盖几何相关的性能变化，并导致关于模型泛化能力的误导性结论。本文旨在揭示并解决这一问题。

Method: 识别内在几何（曲率）为影响模型有效性的关键潜变量；提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区间；在14个数据集上评估18个代表性模型（包括GCNs、GFMs和表格学习方法）。

Result: 模型排名在相同曲率区间内高度稳定，但在不同区间间显著变化，表明性能根本上是几何依赖而非普遍可迁移的。特别地，识别出GFMs相较于几何对齐的GNNs存在收益递减的区间。

Conclusion: 提出几何感知评估协议，相比标准聚合基准能提供更可靠和可解释的比较。发布代码、曲率分层数据集拆分和评估工具以支持可复现的未来评估。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [382] [Comparing Sentiment Contagion in AI-Agent and Human Social Networks: Evidence from MOLTBOOK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06665)
*Elyes Ben chaabane,Savindu Herath,Yash Raj Shrestha*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 研究了一个纯AI社交网络MOLTBOOK中近290万条帖子和150万条评论，发现负面帖子吸引更多回复，但回复通常趋于中性，负面情绪不会在数天内扩散，AI网络表现出情绪中和而非情绪循环的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体之间的交互中情绪传播的模式，尤其是负面情绪是否像人类社交网络中那样扩散，以及回复是否具有安抚作用。

Method: 研究了由自主语言模型智能体组成的社交网络MOLTBOOK，分析了约290万条帖子和150万条评论的情绪倾向及其随时间的变化。

Result: 负面帖子获得的回复显著多于中性或正面帖子；但回复通常会变为中性情绪；几乎没有证据表明负面情绪会在数天内扩散。

Conclusion: AI智能体网络的行为模式与人类社交网络不同：它们倾向于抑制情绪极端化，呈现“负面关注后情绪中和”的模式，而非负面情绪循环。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to interact not only with people, but also with one another. We investigate what happens to sentiment in such an AI-only social network: does negativity spread, or do replies calm it down? We study MOLTBOOK, a social network made up of autonomous language-model agents, using almost 2.9 million posts and 1.5 million comments. Negative posts receive many more replies than neutral or positive posts, so negativity still attracts attention. However, replies to negative content usually do not stay negative. They most often become neutral, and there is meager evidence that negative sentiment spreads across days. The main pattern is therefore not a cycle of negativity, but negative attention followed by neutralisation. These findings suggest that AI-agent networks may behave differently from human social networks: they may dampen emotional extremes, while still depending strongly on how interactions are organised.

</details>


### [383] [Technological Fitness and Regional Growth in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07202)
*Rintaro Karashima,Hiroyasu Inoue*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用日本47个都道府县1981-2015年约390万条企业专利数据，通过Fitness-Complexity算法构建区域技术能力指标，发现区域技术复杂度（Fitness）与后续五年人均实际GRP增长率呈显著正相关（β=0.0029, p=0.007），且该效应在初始收入较低的地区更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究区域技术能力的 sophistication（复杂度）与经济增长之间的关系，尤其是在日本都道府县层面的影响机制。

Method: 使用约390万条企业专利数据，构建47个都道府县与35个技术类别之间的二部网络，应用Fitness-Complexity算法计算五个七年期（1981-2015）的区域Fitness得分；采用含Driscoll-Kraay标准误的固定效应面板模型，以未来五年实际人均GRP年均增长率为因变量。

Result: Prefectural Fitness与后续经济增长显著正相关（β=0.0029, p=0.007），但仅在同时包含个体与时间固定效应时显著；截面相关性在不同时期符号会变化；Fitness的增长效应在初始收入较低的地区更强；滞后/领先分析支持Fitness→增长的单向因果关系。

Conclusion: 区域技术复杂度是经济增长的重要预测因子，尤其在经济发展水平较低、增长空间更大的地区作用更显著，且该关系具有单向因果性。

Abstract: Technological knowledge plays an important role in shaping regional economic performance. This study examines the relationship between the sophistication of regional technological capabilities and economic growth across Japanese prefectures. Using approximately 3.9 million corporate patent records filed from fiscal years 1981 to 2015, we construct bipartite networks linking 47 prefectures to 35 technology classes and apply the Fitness-Complexity algorithm to derive regional Fitness scores for seven five-year periods. We estimate fixed-effects panel models with Driscoll-Kraay standard errors, using the annual average growth rate of real gross regional product per capita over the subsequent five years as the dependent variable. Prefectural Fitness is positively associated with subsequent growth ($\hatβ = 0.0029$, $p = 0.007$) after controlling for initial income, population density, and patenting activity, but this relationship is detectable only when both entity and time fixed effects are included. Cross-sectional correlations between Fitness and subsequent growth change sign across periods, underscoring the importance of the panel approach. The growth effect of Fitness is stronger in prefectures with lower initial income, suggesting that technological sophistication contributes more to growth where there is greater scope for economic expansion. Lag and lead analyses indicate that the relationship runs from Fitness to subsequent growth rather than the reverse.

</details>


### [384] [Information Networks of Stock Prices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07450)
*Muhammad Aldy Hassan,Hokky Situngkir*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用2015-2025年印尼资本市场的2328个滚动窗口数据，系统比较了Pearson相关与互信息(MI)在三种依赖估计器、两种图过滤方案(MST/PMFG)和四种社区解码器组合下的24种配置。结果表明：Pearson、MST与Infomap组合在恢复传统行业分类上最为稳健；PMFG在揭示局部结构和异质社群方面更优；MI自适应分箱在残差信息检测中优于kNN；MI与PMFG的协同虽未取代线性相关的主导地位，但为挖掘超越正式行业边界的隐藏经济子结构（如大宗商品体制的凝聚力）提供了关键分析视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票价格的集体运动蕴含复杂相互依赖关系，传统方法仅通过线性视角简化处理，无法充分捕捉市场中的非线性结构和隐藏经济子结构。

Method: 在2015-2025年间2328个滚动观测窗口上，测试了3种依赖估计器（Pearson、MI自适应分箱、MI-kNN）× 2种图过滤方案（MST、PMFG）× 4种社区解码器的24种方法学配置，系统比较其在印尼资本市场结构性网络表示中的表现。

Result: (1) Pearson+MST+Infomap在恢复传统行业分类中最稳健；(2) PMFG在揭示局部结构和异质社群方面更优；(3) MI自适应分箱在残差信息检测中比kNN更有效，直方图正则化能抑制经验噪声而不丢失非线性依赖痕迹；(4) MI与PMFG的协同不能取代线性相关的主导地位，但可挖掘超越正式行业边界的隐藏经济子结构。

Conclusion: 拓扑丰富度并不总能与行业分类精度共振；线性相关仍是恢复传统行业分类的最稳健基础，而MI与PMFG组合作为补充性分析工具，在揭示跨部门隐藏经济关联（如大宗商品体制）方面具有独特价值。

Abstract: The collective movement of stock prices harbors complex interdependencies that are conventionally simplified only through a linear lens. This paper explores computed structural network representations in the Indonesian capital market by testing the limits of Pearson correlation and Mutual Information (MI) in unveiling the spectral dynamics of the market. Across 2,328 rolling observation windows from 2015 to 2025, we examine 24 methodological configurations that combine three dependency estimators (Pearson, MI adaptive binning, and MI-kNN), two graph filtering schemes (Minimum Spanning Tree/MST and Planar Maximally Filtered Graph/PMFG), and four community decoders.
  The empirical results unveil a fundamental reality: topological richness does not always resonate with sectoral classification precision. The Pearson, MST, and Infomap configuration is shown to remain the most robust foundation for recovering conventional sectoral taxonomy. Nevertheless, when deeper observation demands the exposition of local structures and the weave of heterogeneous communities, the architectural relaxation through PMFG demonstrates its superiority. In the realm of residual information detection, MI adaptive binning appears far more proportional than kNN; histogram-based regularization successfully tames empirical noise without sweeping away traces of non-linear dependency. Ultimately, the synergy of MI and PMFG is not positioned to dethrone the dominance of linear correlation, but rather to provide an essential analytical lens for excavating hidden economic sub-structures -- such as the cohesion of commodity regimes -- that have long transcended the rigid boundaries of the market's formal sectors.

</details>


### [385] [What Causes COVID-19 Fear? General Drivers of Fear During a Health Crisis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20146)
*Daniele Baccega,Paolo Castagno,Antonio Fernández Anta,Juan Marcos Ramirez,Matteo Sereno*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用德尔菲美国CTIS调查数据，通过概率因果模型分析了新冠疫情中人口变量、流行病学条件和信息源暴露对恐惧情绪的影响。结果发现信息源比人口和流行病学变量更能解释恐惧的差异；经因果调整后，机构性和专家驱动的信息源与更高的恐惧相关，而政治家、宗教领袖和替代信息渠道则与较低的恐惧相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新冠疫情不仅引发全球健康危机，还导致了信息疫情（infodemic），不同来源的信息暴露影响了公众的情绪反应。作者希望探究哪些因素决定了个体对感染的自报恐惧水平，特别是不同信息源的作用。

Method: 使用德尔菲美国CTIS调查数据，引入概率因果模型（Probabilistic Causal Model）估计因果关系强度，并通过平均处理效应（ATE）量化不同信息源对恐惧的因果影响，同时进行因果调整。

Result: 信息源暴露对恐惧方差的解释比例大于人口变量和流行病学变量。经因果调整后，机构性和专家驱动的信息源增加了恐惧水平，而政治家、宗教领袖和替代信息渠道则降低了恐惧水平。

Conclusion: 信息生态系统在塑造公共卫生危机中的情绪反应方面起核心作用，因果推断方法对于研究疫情中的行为反应具有重要价值。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic triggered not only a global health crisis but also an infodemic, where exposure to heterogeneous information sources influenced public emotional responses. In this work, we investigate the determinants of self-reported fear of infection using data from the Delphi US CTIS survey. In particular, we analyze how demographic variables, epidemiological conditions, and exposure to different information sources shape fear levels. We introduce a Probabilistic Causal Model to estimate causal relationship strengths, identifying the variables that most strongly influence fear. Our results indicate that exposure to information sources accounts for a greater proportion of the variance in fear than demographic and epidemiological variables do. We further compute the Average Treatment Effect to quantify the impact of different information sources on fear. After causal adjustment, institutional and expert-driven sources are associated with increased fear levels, whereas politicians, religious leaders, and alternative information channels are associated with reduced fear. These findings highlight both the central role of the information ecosystem in shaping emotional responses during public health crises and the value of causal inference approaches for studying behavioral responses to pandemics.

</details>


### [386] [Popularity Feedback Constrains Innovation in Cultural Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09997)
*Lucas Gautheron,Raja Marjieh,Dalton C. Conley,Seth Frey,Hannah Rubin,Mike D. Schneider,Ofer Tchernichovski,Nori Jacoby*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 通过大规模在线实验（N=1008）发现，流行度反馈会降低文化多样性、减缓创新速度，并影响选择与创造两个阶段：选择时产生累积优势效应，创造时减少颠覆性改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解流行度反馈如何影响集体创造力的动态过程，特别是社会反馈循环（选择与创造之间的相互作用）对文化创新的影响机制。

Method: 大规模在线实验，1008名参与者在演化市场中迭代选择图片并产生自己的修改版本，通过操纵流行度信息的可见性来研究其影响。

Result: 流行度反馈降低了文化多样性、延迟了美学改进；选择阶段产生累积优势，创造阶段参与者做出更少颠覆性改变并倾向于扩展已有视觉模式。

Conclusion: 文化市场中的反馈循环不仅塑造选择行为，也直接或间接地影响文化创新的形式和方向。

Abstract: Real-world creative processes ranging from art to science rely on social feedback-loops between selection and creation. Yet, the effects of popularity feedback on collective creativity remain poorly understood. We investigate how popularity ratings influence cultural dynamics in a large-scale online experiment where participants ($N = 1\,008$) iteratively \textit{select} images from evolving markets and \textit{produce} their own modifications. Results show that exposing the popularity of images reduces cultural diversity and slows innovation, delaying aesthetic improvements. Popularity feedback is associated with changes to both selection and creative stages. During selection, popularity information triggers cumulative advantage, with participants preferentially building upon popular images, reducing diversity. During creation, participants make less disruptive changes, and are more likely to expand existing visual patterns. Feedback loops in cultural markets thus not only shape selection, but also, directly or indirectly, the form and direction of cultural innovation.

</details>


### [387] [Two-Path Operators, Triadic Decompositions, and Majorized Quotients for Ego-Centered Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10258)
*Moses Boudourides*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"wedge"（两路径）的算子框架，将Burt的结构洞理论与ego网络联系起来，通过两漫步矩阵分解为三角边支撑部分和非边支撑部分，研究了商/压缩构造下的两漫步传递定理，并在十个基准图上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两路径是聚类、三角闭合、冗余和经纪等网络现象的基础组合对象，但缺乏统一的算子化处理框架。作者希望建立一种严格的数学形式化方法，将结构洞理论与节点中心ego网络联系起来。

Method: (1) 建立wedge入射诱导的典型"两漫步"矩阵及唯一分解（边支撑三角部分与非边支撑开放部分）；(2) 研究商/压缩构造以压缩主导ego网络集合及选定的"穿越"节点；(3) 证明压缩下的两漫步传递定理，建立带显式非负误差项的不等式，以及用wedge-均衡划分刻画等式条件。

Result: 提出了两漫步传递定理，证明在压缩下存在一个带有显式非负误差项的不等式，并给出了等式成立的充要条件（wedge-均衡划分）。在十个基准图及其ego-穿越压缩上通过表格驱动诊断验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该工作为结构洞和网络压缩提供了严格的数学基础，两漫步传递定理及wedge-均衡划分的条件为网络分析中的压缩操作提供了理论保证，在基准图上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Two-paths (wedges) are the elementary combinatorial objects behind clustering, triadic closure, redundancy, and brokerage. Motivated by a two-path formalism that links Burt's structural holes to node-centered ego networks, we develop an operator viewpoint in which wedge incidence induces a canonical ``two-walk'' matrix and a unique decomposition into an edge--supported (triadic) part and a nonedge-supported (open) part. We then study quotient/contraction constructions designed to compress collections of dominating ego networks together with selected ``traversing'' nodes, and we prove a two--walk transfer theorem under contraction, establishing an inequality with an explicit nonnegative error term and an equality characterization in terms of a wedge--equitable partition. Finally, we illustrate the theory on ten benchmark graphs and their ego-traversing contractions using table-driven diagnostics.

</details>


### [388] [Missing Links in Public Email and Covert Networks: A Comparative Evaluation of Link Prediction, Hyperlink Prediction, and ERGM Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22606)
*Moses Boudourides*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了链路预测（LP）、超链路预测（HP）和指数随机图模型（ERGM）在部分观测网络中的缺失推理能力，发现LP在二元组恢复上表现强劲，HP（尤其是CHESHIRE）在推断高阶群结构时更具优势，ERGM则提供了可解释的依赖性补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对链路预测、超链路预测和ERGM在部分观测网络中进行缺失推理时的系统性比较，尤其缺少在确保可比性的统一掩码协议下的评估。

Method: 采用统一的掩码协议移除由被持有的超链接诱导的二元证据，在公开电子邮件和隐蔽网络数据集上，以二元链路预测为基线，对比经典启发式方法、基于提升二元分数的超链路预测方法以及CHESHIRE（切比雪夫谱超链接预测器），同时引入ERGM条件边际概率作为可解释基准。

Result: LP在二元组恢复上表现强劲；HP特别是CHESHIRE在推断高阶群结构时提供增益；ERGM通过条件边际概率提供可解释的依赖补充。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了比较性、可复现的评估框架，厘清了在存在网络缺失数据时，LP、HP和ERGM各自最适用的场景。

Abstract: We study missing-link inference in partially observed networks by systematically comparing dyadic link prediction (LP) with hyperlink prediction (HP) and an estimation-based ERGM comparator. LP serves as the primary baseline, using classical heuristics computed on the observed graph. HP extends this framework by scoring candidate higher-order structures (cliques) via lifted dyadic scores and via the CHEbyshev Spectral HyperlInk pREdictor (CHESHIRE). All methods are evaluated under a common masking protocol that removes dyadic evidence induced by held-out hyperlinks to ensure comparability. Across public email and covert-network datasets, LP remains strong for dyadic recovery, while HP -- particularly CHESHIRE -- provides gains when the inferential target is higher-order group structure. ERGMs offer an interpretable dependence-based complement through conditional tie probabilities. The contribution is a comparative, reproducible evaluation clarifying when LP, HP, and ERGM estimation are most appropriate under network missingness.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [Fixed point compositionality via low-rank gluing rules in inhibition-dominated threshold-linear networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07336)
*Juliana Londono Alvarez*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过低秩粘合（low-rank gluings）的形式化方法，严格证明了在抑制主导的阈值线性网络（TLNs）中，结构模块性如何支撑功能组合性：全局不动点局部不动点的组合约束，并展示了如何利用这一机制工程设计具有组合吸引子动力学的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑能在稳定结构和有限资源下产生高度灵活复杂的行为，组合性（compositionality）是其关键机制。尽管网络模块性常与组合性相关联，但在非线性网络中缺乏严格的数学刻画。

Method: 提出低秩粘合（low-rank gluings）构建模块化网络，其中子网络通过特定低秩耦合连接。对于rank-1粘合子类，给出全局不动点与局部不动点组合的完整刻画。将结果应用于图基网络，从CTLNs推广至gCTLNs。

Result: 证明了全局不动点约束为局部不动点的组合；在rank-1粘合中完整确定了哪些局部不动点组合能成为全局不动点；将不动点分解规则从CTLNs推广到gCTLNs，证明其鲁棒性；展示了粘合规则可用于工程化构建具有组合数量可预测吸引子的网络，包括不动点组合和组合极限环。

Conclusion: 结构模块性通过低秩粘合机制严格支撑功能组合性，为理解生物和人工神经网络中的组合动力学提供了数学基础，并给出了可操作的工程设计方法。

Abstract: Brains routinely generate highly flexible and complex behaviors on a relatively stable structure and limited resources. A key mechanism underlying this ability is compositionality, which allows the brain to efficiently decompose complex tasks into simpler, reusable primitives. While network modularity has often been linked to compositionality in biological and artificial networks, a rigorous mathematical characterization of this relationship in nonlinear networks is still lacking. In this work, we formally investigate how structural modularity supports functional compositionality in inhibition-dominated threshold-linear networks (TLNs). We introduce a novel class of modular network assembly called low-rank gluings, where component subnetworks with arbitrary internal connectivity are connected via specific low-rank couplings. We prove that the global fixed points of these networks are constrained to be combinations of the local fixed points of their constituent modules. For a more structured subclass, called rank-1 gluings, we provide a complete characterization that determines which combinations of local fixed points yield global ones. We apply these results to graph-based networks, extending fixed point decomposition rules from combinatorial threshold-linear networks (CTLNs) to the more flexible family of generalized CTLNs (gCTLNs), thereby proving that these structural rules are more robust than initially posited. Finally, we demonstrate that these gluing rules provide a mathematically tractable recipe for engineering compositional dynamics, enabling the construction of networks with a combinatorially large repertoire of predictable attractors that can be understood from simpler component motifs, ranging from compositions of fixed points to compositional limit cycles.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [390] [Learning Fair Demand Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06830)
*Adam N. Elmachtoub,Hyemi Kim,Jonathan Y. Tan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究数据驱动定价中的公平性问题，通过构建包含需求估计和价格优化的两阶段决策模型，分析了在不同公平性约束（均等主义和罗尔斯主义）下，将公平性施加于估计阶段或优化阶段对社会福利和消费者福利的影响。研究发现：在均等主义公平下，两种策略的社会福利比较取决于市场特征；在罗尔斯主义公平下，两种策略完全等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动定价在航空、贷款、保险和零售等领域广泛应用，但可能产生歧视性结果引发公平性担忧。现有研究缺乏对定价流水线中何时何地引入公平性考量及其对社会影响的系统性理解。

Method: 构建了一个两阶段决策模型：第一阶段是线性需求模型估计，第二阶段是基于估计结果的价格优化。分别考虑均等主义公平（组间训练损失/价格/需求差异最小化）和罗尔斯主义公平（最小化最差组的表现）。比较两类策略：在需求估计阶段施加公平约束，或在价格优化阶段施加公平约束。

Result: 1) 训练损失均等化会导致多解且可能产生不良结果；2) 均等主义公平下，当数据集中的市场规模和价格相近时，价格公平在估计阶段施加更有利于消费者，需求公平在优化阶段施加带来更好的消费者结果；3) 罗尔斯主义公平下，两种策略完全等价；4) 通过真实疫苗定价数据进行了案例验证。

Conclusion: 定价流水线中公平性施加的阶段选择至关重要，不同公平性概念和不同市场条件下，最优策略不同。为实践者提供了在不同场景下选择公平性干预点的理论指导。

Abstract: Data-driven pricing is increasingly prevalent in sectors such as airlines, lending, insurance, and retail. By learning demand models from customer features and setting prices accordingly, these systems may generate discriminatory outcomes that raise fairness concerns. This leads to fundamental questions - how and where should systems incorporate fairness considerations in the pricing pipeline, and how does it ultimately affect societal outcomes? To answer these, we study a stylized model where a seller has a two-stage decision pipeline comprising linear demand model estimation followed by price optimization. The seller considers fairness notions in training loss, price, and demand, under both parity-wise and Rawlsian perspectives.
  We show that equalizing training loss across consumer groups leads to multiple solutions, which in turn can result in undesirable outcomes despite being a standard approach in fair machine learning. Focusing instead on fairness applied directly to prices or demand, we compare two strategies that enforce fairness in either the demand estimation stage or the price optimization stage. For parity-wise fairness, we characterize when each strategy yields higher social welfare under small fairness levels. We show that when market sizes and prices in the dataset are similar, imposing price fairness in the estimation stage is more beneficial to consumers, whereas imposing demand fairness in the optimization stage yields better consumer outcomes. For Rawlsian fairness, the two strategies coincide exactly. Lastly, we extend our model to alternate demand functions and conduct a case study using real-world vaccine pricing data.

</details>


### [391] [Toward a Metaphysics of Learning Analytics: Ontological Positioning of Data, Inference, and Normativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06851)
*Kensuke Takii*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过回溯学习分析（LA）自身的定义与原则，构建了LA的形而上学体系，回答了“LA是什么”的本体论问题，揭示了数据存在方式、八类本体论前提主体，并通过实然/应然问题澄清LA不从数据推导规范，特别指出“规范嵌入型LA”实践与第一原则存在本体论张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LA领域发展迅速，但关于其哲学基础的讨论中，认识论和伦理学层面的辩论热烈，而形而上学层面的探讨却严重不足，缺乏从内在原则出发推导LA自身身份认同的努力。

Method: 通过回溯LA自身的定义与原则，从内部推导答案。具体包括：厘清LA所操作数据的存在方式、识别包括学习者在内的八类本体论前提主体、利用实然/应然问题澄清LA不从数据中推导规范。

Result: 揭示了“规范嵌入型LA”（norm-embedded LA）这类实践混淆了LA的目的与操作，与第一原则产生本体论张力；建立了从LA内部浮现而非外部强加的形而上学体系。

Conclusion: 该形而上学体系并非从外部强加于LA，而是揭示了LA自身一直隐含预设的内容，为LA的身份认同提供了内在的哲学基础。

Abstract: The Learning Analytics (LA) community has undergone rapid development over the 15 years since the first LAK conference was held. However, while epistemological and ethical debates regarding the philosophical foundations of LA have been vigorous, metaphysical discussions have been sparse, signifying a lack of effort to derive the identity of LA from its internal principles. In this paper, we attempt to establish a metaphysics of LA by addressing the ontological question of ``What is LA?'' We do so by tracing back to LA's own definitions and principles to derive an answer from within LA itself. Specifically, we address what kind of existence the data LA operates on constitutes, identify eight agents including learners as ontological prerequisites, and clarify, via the is/ought problem, that LA does not derive norms from data. In particular, this system reveals that a class of LA practices, here termed \textit{norm-embedded LA}, conflates LA's purpose with its operations, creating an ontological tension with the first principle. We also discuss connections with related fields and the limitations of this system. The metaphysics outlined here is not imposed from outside LA, but surfaces what LA itself has always implicitly presupposed.

</details>


### [392] [AI Sovereignty: A Qualitative Model of Strategic Competition as AI Becomes an Instrument of National Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07245)
*Timothy Clancy,Asmeret Naugle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI主权的定义及首个微观、中观、宏观多层次定性模型，分析了国家间AI战略竞争的关键杠杆点（加速器、电力、水、数据集、技能劳动力）及其直接动能（如无人机打击数据中心）和间接非动能（网络、太空、信息、经济胁迫、外交）作用方式，预测了AI驱动国家权力的竞争动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI主权日益成为国家战略竞争的核心，但其成本巨大且缺乏明确的定义和概念模型来指导相关动态分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了AI主权的操作性定义，并构建了首个涵盖微观、中观、宏观三个层次的定性模型，基于模型进行定性预测分析。

Result: 识别出加速器、电力、水、数据集和技能劳动力等关键杠杆点；区分了直接动能（如伊朗用无人机攻击数据中心）和间接非动能（网络、太空、信息、经济胁迫、外交）两种作用方式。

Conclusion: 若假设成立，AI主权竞争将成为21世纪国家提升经济状况、竞争优势和整体国力的核心驱动力。

Abstract: AI sovereignty is the extent to which a nation independently controls its artificial intelligence (AI) technologies. The race toward ever-more-sophisticated frontier AI models is of increasing strategic importance, with nations considering how AI might improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power. However, the costs of AI sovereignty are enormous, and we lack definitions and conceptual models to navigate evolving AI sovereignty dynamics. We address this gap with definitions relevant to AI sovereignty, along with a first-of-its-kind qualitative model that incorporates micro, meso, and macro contributors. Model-based qualitative forecasts highlight competitive dynamics and evolving potential for AI-driven national power. The model identifies key leverage points that nations can use to enhance their own growth or degrade an adversary's, including consideration of accelerators, electricity, water, data sets and skilled workforce. These leverage points can be activated at strategic and operational levels through both direct kinetic actions, such as Iran's targeting of data centers with drones, and indirect non-kinetic effects including cyber, space, information, economic coercion and diplomacy. If our assumptions and hypotheses are valid, this strategic competition may come to define how nations improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power in the 21st Century.

</details>


### [393] [Sustainability by Design in Decentralized Autonomous Organizations: An Empirical Review of Governance, Innovation, and Institutional Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05667)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究比较了由DAO治理的ERC-8004标准与由企业联盟治理的Google A2A标准，通过LLM驱动的比较分析管道研究去中心化组织中的创新治理与可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经济学创新理论基于层级制企业和封闭组织边界的假设，无法解释去中心化、数字原生组织中的创新过程。DAOs作为一种新兴创新生态系统，将可持续性嵌入组织设计，但对其治理机制理解有限。

Method: 采用LLM驱动的比较分析管道，集成自动化标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对ERC-8004（DAO治理）和Google A2A（企业联盟治理）的治理话语进行大规模分析。

Result: 对比了两种治理模式下的社会技术权力结构，揭示了去中心化与中心化治理在创新标准制定中的差异。

Conclusion: 为学者、政策制定者和设计者提供了关于创新、技术治理与可持续性在未来组织形式中如何对齐的循证洞见。

Abstract: Recent innovation theories on economics remain largely grounded in assumptions of hierarchical firms and closed organizational boundaries, offering limited insight into how innovation unfolds within decentralized, digitally native organizations. Decentralized Autonomous Organizations (DAOs) represent an emerging form of innovation ecosystem characterized by blockchain-based transparency, open participation, and token-driven governance, in which sustainability can be embedded directly into organizational design. This study compares two standards, ERC-8004 and Google A2A, who address the same agent interoperability question, while the former is governed by DAO and the latter by corporation consortium. They are examined through an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures. The study provides evidence-based insights for scholars, policymakers, and designers seeking to align innovation, technological governance, and sustainability in future organizational forms.

</details>


### [394] [Two-Phase Simulated Annealing for Equitable Team Formation: Eliminating Complaints in Large Engineering Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07270)
*Yiwei Sun,Xinru Deng,Dimitrios G Papageorgiou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段算法方法，将偏好满足与公平性优化解耦，应用于大规模工程学生团队组建。第一阶段通过图论聚类形成固定三人组，最大化互选偏好；第二阶段采用模拟退火算法将三人组配对为六人团队，优化GPA方差、性别平衡等公平性指标。在238名学生部署中实现零投诉、GPA方差0.005、94.3%偏好满足率，并在82个历史实例（1,538个团队）上验证了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有团队组建工具要么在优化公平性时忽略学生偏好（如CATME、Team-Anneal），要么在满足偏好时牺牲团队平衡（如自选方式），导致投诉率高达5-35%。需要一种既能满足学生偏好又能实现学术公平和人口多样性的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 两阶段算法：阶段1采用图论聚类将学生形成固定三人组，最大化组内互选偏好以保留社会纽带；阶段2使用模拟退火算法将三人组配对为六人团队，同时优化GPA方差、性别平衡和规模约束。这种分解类似于材料科学中的层次优化过程。

Result: 在238名学生部署中：投诉率从>30%降至零；GPA方差达到0.005（历史均值为9.74）；消除性别孤立个体；偏好满足率达94.3%。在82个历史实例（1,538个团队，6个学年）上的验证确认了显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段方法成功将偏好满足与公平性优化解耦，在不妥协的情况下同时实现两个目标。模拟退火这一材料科学技术在教育管理中的创新应用展示了跨学科方法解决教育挑战的潜力，提供了可扩展、可复制的工程教育团队组建方案。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a novel two-phase algorithmic approach that decouples preference satisfaction from fairness optimization in student team formation, achieving both objectives without compromise. The method applies simulated annealing -- a core materials science technique -- to an educational challenge, demonstrating pedagogical integration of administrative processes. Background: Forming effective teams in large engineering cohorts (100+ students) requires balancing student preferences, academic fairness, and demographic diversity. Existing tools either optimize for fairness while ignoring preferences (CATME, Team-Anneal) or accommodate preferences while compromising balance (self-selection), leaving complaint rates at 5--35%. Intended Outcomes: Eliminate formal complaints, achieve near-zero GPA variance between teams, prevent gender isolation, and maintain high preference satisfaction while creating a scalable, reproducible solution applicable across engineering programs. Application Design: Phase 1 forms fixed triads through graph-theoretic clustering that maximizes mutual preferences, preserving social bonds. Phase 2 employs simulated annealing to pair triads into teams of six while optimizing GPA variance, gender balance, and size constraints. This decomposition mirrors hierarchical optimization in materials processing. Findings: Deployed across 238 students, the algorithm eliminated formal complaints entirely (vs >30% baseline), achieved GPA variance of 0.005 (vs. historical mean 9.74), eliminated gender-isolated individuals, and maintained 94.3% preference satisfaction. Validation against 82 historical grouping instances (1,538 teams, 6 academic years) confirmed significant improvement over conventional methods.

</details>


### [395] [Measuring Agents in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04123)
*Melissa Z. Pan,Negar Arabzadeh,Riccardo Cogo,Yuxuan Zhu,Alexander Xiong,Lakshya A Agrawal,Huanzhi Mao,Emma Shen,Sid Pallerla,Liana Patel,Shu Liu,Tianneng Shi,Xiaoyuan Liu,Jared Quincy Davis,Emmanuele Lacavalla,Alessandro Basile,Shuyi Yang,Paul Castro,Daniel Kang,Koushik Sen,Dawn Song,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia,Marquita Ellis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了生产中LLM智能体的现状，通过对20个案例深度访谈和86个部署系统的调查，发现生产级智能体倾向于使用简单、可控的方法：68%在10步内需人工干预，70%依赖提示工程而非权重微调，74%主要依靠人工评估。可靠性是最大挑战，从业者通过系统级设计来应对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体已在多个行业投入生产使用，但学术界对其成功部署的技术方法缺乏系统性理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供生产环境中智能体部署的真实全貌。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：20个深度访谈案例研究 + 86个已部署系统的从业者问卷调查，覆盖26个领域。从构建动机、构建方式、评估方法及开发挑战四个维度进行分析。

Result: 生产智能体呈现三大特征：1) 68%执行不超过10步就需要人工干预；2) 70%依赖对现有模型进行提示工程而非模型权重微调；3) 74%主要依赖人工评估。可靠性（随时间保持一致正确行为）是首要开发挑战。

Conclusion: MAP研究记录了生产级智能体的当前状态，揭示了工业界与学术界之间的认知差距，为研究社区指明了尚未充分探索的研究方向，即可靠性保障和系统级设计方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents already operate in production across many industries, yet we lack an understanding of what technical methods make deployments successful. We present the first systematic study of Measuring Agents in Production, MAP, using first-hand data from agent developers. We conducted 20 case studies via in-depth interviews and surveyed 86 deployed systems practitioners across 26 domains. We investigate why organizations build agents, how they build them, how they evaluate them, and their top development challenges. Our study finds that production agents are built using simple, controllable approaches: 68% execute at most 10 steps before human intervention, 70% rely on prompting off-the-shelf models instead of weight tuning, and 74% depend primarily on human evaluation. Reliability (consistent correct behavior over time) remains the top development challenge, which practitioners currently address through systems-level design. MAP documents the current state of production agents, providing the research community with visibility into deployment realities and underexplored research avenues.

</details>


### [396] [Training for Technology: Adoption and Productive Use of Generative AI in Legal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04982)
*Benjamin M. Chen,Hong Bao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过随机实验研究法律专业学生使用生成式AI的效果，发现未经培训的LLM访问会降低表现，而简短培训干预能显著提升成绩，且培训主要通过提高采纳率而非使用效果发挥作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究针对性用户培训是否能释放生成式AI在专业环境中的生产力潜力。

Method: 164名法律专业学生随机分为三组：无GenAI访问组、可选LLM访问组、LLM访问+简短培训组，完成问题识别考试。采用主分层分析（Principal Stratification Analysis）检验培训的作用机制。

Result: 未经培训的LLM访问产生反效果：答案更短、案例陈述错误更多、分数略低。培训组采纳率更高（41% vs 26%），分数高0.27个等级分，适用规则更准确。主分层分析表明培训主要通过促进采纳而非提升使用效果起作用。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的生产力增益需要同时投资于访问权限和教学培训；反驳了GenAI主要惠及低技能劳动者的观点，因为未经培训时高能力者选择不使用，低能力者使用却效果不佳。

Abstract: Can targeted user training unlock the productive potential of generative artificial intelligence in professional settings? We study this question using a randomized experiment in which 164 law students completed an issue-spotting examination under one of three conditions: no GenAI access, optional access to a large language model (LLM), or LLM access with a brief training intervention.
  Untrained LLM access proved counterproductive: relative to participants without any LLM access, untrained users wrote significantly shorter answers, committed more case misstatements, and scored marginally lower, though most differences fall short of conventional significance. Training reversed this pattern. Trained participants adopted the LLM at higher rates (41% vs. 26%; p = 0.044), scored 0.27 grade points higher than untrained users--roughly one fine grade--(p = 0.027), and stated applicable rules more accurately (p = 0.014).
  Principal stratification analysis suggests training operates primarily through adoption rather than effectiveness--the adoption lower bound (1.06) exceeds the effectiveness upper bound (0.42) at strict mean dominance--though confidence intervals are wide.
  More broadly, these findings challenge the view that GenAI primarily benefits lower-skilled workers: without training, higher-ability practitioners opt out while lower-ability users adopt but unproductively. Realizing GenAI's productivity gains requires investment in both access and instruction.

</details>


### [397] [Benchmarking LLMs for Community Governance Simulation with Life-history Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23783)
*Xu Chen,Yuanzi Li,Lei Wang,Nan Lu,Yang Wang,Anding Wang,Lei Shi,Xiaoxing Fu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对社区治理的个性化居民模拟框架，包含数据集、基准测试、算法和系统四个组成部分。通过收集92位居民的半结构化访谈数据（约120万字），研究发现了18个主流LLM在不同提示策略下的表现。提出的curriculum-LoRA算法以约10倍成本降低实现了与最强基线相当的保真度，最终集成为闭环政策评估流水线，使资源有限的基层行政机构能够在真实部署前进行系统性数字预评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有使用LLM模拟人类受访者的研究通常仅用少数人口统计变量（年龄、性别、收入）提示模型，只能模拟一般角色类型，不足以支持社区治理中依赖具体居民观点的决策需求。研究旨在弥合这一差距，实现面向特定居民的个性化模拟。

Method: 研究构建了一个四部分框架：(1) 数据集：对92位城市社区居民进行两小时半结构化访谈，收集约120万字第一人称叙事，涵盖九个社区治理领域；(2) 基准测试：在18个主流LLM上评估四种提示策略，发现加入丰富生活史档案可提升保真度但增加成本；(3) 算法：提出curriculum-LoRA参数高效个性化框架，弥合保真度与成本之间的差距；(4) 系统：将curriculum-LoRA集成到闭环政策评估流水线中。

Result: 加入丰富生活史档案的提示策略在各LLM上比无档案基线显著提升模拟保真度，但带来更高的输入token成本。curriculum-LoRA算法以约10倍更低的每次调用成本达到了最强基线的保真度水平，在所有测试配置中实现Pareto最优。

Conclusion: 该研究使基于LLM的个体级居民模拟对资源受限的基层行政机构成为可行，支持社区治理决策在实际部署前进行系统性数字预评估。

Abstract: Effective community governance hinges on understanding what specific residents think and need. Recent work has used large language models (LLMs) to simulate human respondents, offering a scalable, reproducible way to study human attitudes and behaviors at low cost. However, these studies typically prompt the model with just a few demographic variables (age, gender, income), simulating only general role types. This is insufficient for community governance, where decisions depend on the views of specific residents. We bridge this gap with an integrated research framework covering dataset, benchmark, algorithm, and system. The dataset comprises approximately 1.2 million characters of first-person narrative collected through two-hour semi-structured interviews with each of 92 residents in an urban community, organized around nine community-governance domains. The benchmark probes 18 mainstream LLMs across four prompting strategies and shows that adding rich life-history profiles meaningfully raises fidelity above the no-profile baseline, but this gain comes with more input tokens per call from the longer prompts they require. The algorithm, curriculum-LoRA, is a parameter-efficient personalization framework that, by closing this fidelity-cost gap, matches the strongest baseline's fidelity at roughly 10x lower per-call cost and Pareto-dominates every configuration tested. The system integrates curriculum-LoRA into a closed-loop policy-evaluation pipeline. Together, these results bring individual-level LLM-based resident simulation within reach of resource-constrained local administrations, enabling community-governance decisions to be systematically pre-evaluated in silico before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [398] [Beyond Tool Adoption: A Practical Five-Stage Developmental Continuum for AI Literacy in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00038)
*J. Paul Liu,Rachel Levy*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出五阶段AI素养连续体模型（从“未参与”到“改进”），帮助高等教育诊断学生AI使用的发展阶段，并通过北卡罗来纳州立大学的实践案例（330+参与者）验证了从回避或盲目使用向知情、批判性使用转变的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI素养框架虽然定义了关键能力，但缺乏对学生AI使用起点及发展路径的诊断指导；学生在AI工具使用上呈现出两极分化（因恐惧/伦理担忧而回避，或盲目依赖），需要一种发展性框架来引导渐进式、负责任的AI素养培养。

Method: 提出了五阶段AI素养连续体（0-4级），与UNESCO和OECD国际框架对齐；在北卡罗来纳州立大学开展基于设计的实施，通过学分课程和高强度工作坊在2024年秋季至2026年春季期间覆盖330+参与者，采用观察和实践导向的方式收集数据。

Result: 参与者表现出从非参与或盲目使用向知情参与过渡的行为；持续且融入学科的经验产生了更明显的批判性评估和改进导向实践的实证；但缺乏经过验证的前/后测工具和对照组，结果属于观察性和实践性的。

Conclusion: AI素养应被视为一种发展性能力，而非单纯的工具采纳；连续的、融入学科的课程体验对培养批判性评估和改进导向的AI素养更为有效；需要进一步开发评估工具和考虑公平机会问题。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) literacy is increasingly recognized as a foundational competency for all university graduates. Yet students' engagement with AI tools often clusters at two extremes: avoidance driven by fear, mistrust, ethical concern, or lack of access, and uncritical reliance that produces fluent output while masking misunderstanding. Existing AI literacy frameworks provide valuable competency definitions, but most offer limited guidance for diagnosing where learners begin and how they progress toward responsible, critical engagement. This paper proposes a five-stage AI Literacy Continuum: 0) Not Yet Engaged, 1) Uncritical Use, 2) Informed Use, 3) Critical Evaluation, and 4) Improvement --that describes developmental orientations toward AI use in higher education. The continuum complements dimensional frameworks by providing educators with a practical diagnostic and instructional pathway aligned with international frameworks, including UNESCO and OECD. We present a design-based implementation case from North Carolina State University, where credit-bearing courses and intensive hands-on workshops engaged more than 330 participants between Fall 2024 and Spring 2026. Because the implementation did not use a validated pre/post instrument or comparison group, we frame the findings as observational and practice-based: participants exhibited behaviors consistent with movement from non-engagement or uncritical use toward informed engagement, while sustained and discipline-embedded experiences produced stronger evidence of critical evaluation and improvement-oriented practice. We discuss curricular pathways, opportunity considerations, assessment strategies, and argue that AI literacy should be understood not as tool adoption alone but as a developmental capacity to understand, evaluate, and responsibly apply AI systems in disciplinary and societal contexts.

</details>


### [399] [Toward Agentic Governance: What Shapes LLM-Agent Intervention in Public Forums?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00603)
*Luyang Zhang,Yi-Yun Chu,Ramayya Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: LLM代理在论坛审核流程中行为存在结构性变异，四种部署选择（模型版本、权重开放状态、服务提供商、系统提示策略）独立影响代理响应，且开放权重与封闭权重模型在可见/隐藏挑战上的拒绝倾向呈现一致差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在公共论坛审核等场景中的应用日益广泛，但其对相同内容返回不同响应的行为变异导致基于代理行为的防御无法可靠复现，亟需系统分析变异的来源与结构性特征。

Method: 识别四种典型部署选择（模型版本、权重开放状态、服务提供商、系统提示策略），跨开放权重和封闭权重模型家族进行面板分析，比较不同部署配置下代理对可见与隐藏挑战的响应差异。

Result: 四种部署选择各自独立且组合地影响代理响应率；封闭权重模型对所有可见挑战的拒绝率更高，开放权重模型则反转此模式或无明显差距；该差异更贴合开放/封闭权重边界而非访问表面。

Conclusion: 可审计的论坛代理治理需要关注所有四种部署选择，而非仅模型名称，因为每个因素均独立影响行为。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used in moderation-relevant public forum workflows, where their choices to answer, acknowledge, repair, or decline are routinely challenged by users, platforms, and regulators. The same agent often returns different responses on identical content, so any defense based on the agent's behavior cannot be reliably reproduced. The variation is structural. Four deployment choices typically invisible to the operator each shift the agent's response rate, and their combinations can produce substantially different interventions on the same forum posts. The four choices are (1) which model version is currently served, which can change between calls without notice; (2) the model's weight-release status (open-weight, with weights publicly downloadable, vs. closed-weight, with weights held by the provider); (3) which provider serves the request; and (4) which system-prompt policy is in force. Across LLMs spanning both open-weight and closed-weight families, we find that the previously reported tendency to decline more on visible than hidden challenges aligns with the open/closed weight boundary in our panel more than with access surface. Every closed-weight cell declines more on visible challenges; every open-weight cell reverses this or shows no gap. Auditable forum-agent governance requires awareness of all four choices, not just the model name, since each independently shifts behavior.

</details>


### [400] [Warning Message Content Increases Help Seeking in a Large-Scale Dark Web CSAM Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06417)
*Caoilte Ó Ciardha,Joel Scanlan,Tegan Insoll,Juha Nurmi,Nina Vaaranen-Valkonen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过在Tor搜索引擎Ahmia.fi上进行大规模现场实验（覆盖近2000万次搜索，其中超300万次涉及CSAM相关词），发现警告信息的内容（特别是危害焦点型信息）显著影响用户点击自助帮助资源的比率，点击率从干预前的8.73%提升至活动期间的15.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警告信息被用于阻止个体搜索儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）并促使其寻求帮助，但关于信息内容效果的大规模现场证据有限，特别是在高匿名性环境中。

Method: 在Tor搜索引擎Ahmia.fi上进行为期140天的现场实验，观测近2000万次搜索，其中超300万次CSAM相关搜索触发警告。用户被随机暴露于不同主题内容和框架的警告信息或中性信息，采用随机对照比较、活动整体分析和中断时间序列模型进行分析。

Result: 所有主动警告信息相比中性条件均提高了帮助资源的点击率，其中危害焦点型信息效果最强。平台层面点击率从干预前的8.73%升至活动期间的15.67%。

Conclusion: 信息内容对警告干预的效果具有重要影响，支持通过优化和调整信息内容来增加用户对帮助资源的参与度。

Abstract: Warning messages have been used to disrupt individuals seeking online child sexual abuse material (CSAM) and promote engagement with support services, yet large-scale field evidence on message content remains limited, particularly in high anonymity environments. This study reports a field experiment on Ahmia.fi, a Tor search engine, examining how warning message content influences behavior. Across a 140-day period, almost 20 million searches were observed, with over 3 million searches containing known CSAM-related terms that triggered a warning linking to an anonymous self-help program. Users were exposed to warning messages varying in thematic content and framing, or a neutral message. Across a randomized comparison, a campaign-wide analysis, and interrupted time series models, message content consistently influenced engagement with help resources. All active messages increased click-through rates to help resources relative to the neutral condition, with a harm-focused message producing the strongest effects. At the platform level, click-through rates increased from 8.73% before the intervention to 15.67% during the campaign. These findings highlight the importance of message content in shaping responses to warning interventions, supporting an approach in which messaging is refined and adapted to increase engagement with support resources.

</details>
